Motivation: 手写数学表达式具有复杂二维布局和长程结构依赖；现有方法常依赖符号级边界框等显式空间监督，标注成本高、扩展性差。

Method: 方法包括三部分：1）RelAST 将 MathML 树压缩为包含符号、空间关系和嵌套深度的三元组序列 [S,R,D]；2）MathVAE 通过符号感知与关系感知的感知正则学习保持结构的潜在表示；3）MathDiT 在结构化潜在空间中进行条件去噪，并通过 AdaLN 注入全局符号计数先验以增强结构一致性。

Result: 实验表明 DiffMath 能生成结构一致的手写数学表达式，性能优于现有方法，并可通过合成数据增强提升下游 OCR 模型准确率。

Conclusion: DiffMath 有效利用 LaTeX/MathML 的层次结构作为先验，降低对位置监督的依赖，在可扩展的手写数学表达式生成与 OCR 数据增强方面具有应用价值。

Abstract: Handwritten Mathematical Expression Generation (HMEG) is challenging due to the complex two-dimensional layouts and long-range structural dependencies of mathematical expressions. Existing methods typically rely on explicit spatial supervision, such as symbol-level bounding boxes, which incurs high annotation costs and limits scalability. In this work, we propose DiffMath, a symbol- and graph-aware latent diffusion framework that leverages the hierarchical structure inherent in LaTeX as a structural prior, eliminating the need for positional supervision. First, we design a Relational Abstract Syntax Tree (RelAST), a generation-oriented representation that distills MathML trees into compact triplet sequences [S, R, D], where each token directly encodes a symbol identity, spatial relation, or nesting depth. Second, we introduce MathVAE, which learns structure-preserving latent representations through symbol-aware and relation-aware perceptual regularization, ensuring that the latent space captures both character semantics and spatial topology. Third, MathDiT performs conditional denoising in this structured latent space, further guided by a global symbol-count prior via Adaptive Layer Normalization (AdaLN) to improve structural coherence. Experiments show that DiffMath produces structurally consistent handwritten expressions, achieves superior performance over existing methods, and improves the accuracy of downstream OCR models through synthetic data augmentation.

</details>


### [37] [Timage: A Generative Text-in-Image Paradigm for Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19944)
*Yifeng Wu,Huimin Huang,Ruiluo Wu,Chunyi Lin,Guanhua Chen,Xian Wu,Wang Song,Ruize Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Timage：将文本查询直接以排版叠加层绘制到图像上，用输入重构而非改模型参数来增强 MLLM 的细粒度空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在细粒度空间推理中常无法将文本查询准确锚定到图像区域；权重改造或冗长提示难以稳定建立语言与视觉坐标的对应关系，并可能损害基础模型能力。

Method: 将多模态理解转化为输入端对齐问题。通过受约束 Schrödinger Bridge（cSB）生成文本叠加层的布局与外观：Region Search 阶段在硬遮挡约束下把噪声运输到与查询相关的图像区域，保护显著前景；Appearance Shaping 阶段用“墨水预算”正则控制字形大小，使文字清晰且视觉均衡。

Result: 在 VMCBench 上，Timage 结合较小的 7B 主干模型超过了更大的专有系统和参数调优基线。

Conclusion: 有意的输入重构可作为一种架构无关、无需改动模型权重的有效手段，显著提升多模态细粒度空间推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often lose track of the right image regions during fine-grained spatial reasoning, because a textual query rarely carries any explicit geometric anchor into the pixel domain. Prevailing remedies either rewire the model's weights or pad the prompt with verbose instructions, yet neither reliably pins the language to the correct visual coordinates without eroding the backbone's general competence. We introduce Timage, a paradigm that recasts multimodal understanding as an alignment problem solved at the input: the query is drawn, as a typeset overlay, onto the image itself. The placement and appearance of this overlay are produced by a Constrained Schrödinger Bridge (cSB), an entropic optimal-transport sampler that factorizes layout synthesis into two coupled stochastic stages. The first stage, Region Search, transports noise toward query-aligned image zones while obeying a hard occlusion barrier that protects salient foreground content; the second stage, Appearance Shaping, sizes the glyphs through an ``ink-budget'' regularizer so that the rendered text stays legible and visually balanced. The resulting overlay behaves as an explicit attention beacon that channels the model's focus along spatial semantics. On the VMCBench suite, Timage paired with a modest 7B backbone clearly overtakes far larger proprietary systems as well as parameter-tuned baselines. The study positions deliberate input reconstruction as a powerful, architecture-neutral lever for strengthening multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [Confidence Calibration for Multimodal LLMs: An Empirical Study through Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19950)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,Ming Kong,Tian Liang,Qiang Long,Bingdi Chen,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析医疗多模态大语言模型中置信度与准确率不一致的问题，并提出结合多策略融合询问与专家LLM评估的校准方法，在三个医学VQA数据集上平均降低40%的ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中，MLLM给出的置信度若与真实正确率不匹配，可能导致错误决策风险。因此需要研究其在医学视觉问答任务中的置信度校准问题，并提升模型输出的可信度。

Method: 提出MS-FBI（Multi-Strategy Fusion-Based Interrogation）方法，通过多种询问策略融合获取更稳健的模型响应与置信信息；同时引入辅助专家LLM进行评估，以实现面向医学领域的置信度校准。

Result: 在三个Medical VQA数据集上的实验显示，该方法将Expected Calibration Error（ECE）平均降低约40%，显著改善了医疗MLLM的置信度校准表现。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，医疗MLLM需要领域特定的置信度校准机制；所提出方法可提升AI辅助诊断系统的可靠性与可信度。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show great potential in medical tasks, but their elicited confidence often misaligns with actual accuracy, potentially leading to misdiagnosis or overlooking correct advice. This study presents the first comprehensive analysis of the relationship between accuracy and confidence in medical MLLMs. It proposes a novel method that combines Multi-Strategy Fusion-Based Interrogation (MS-FBI) with auxiliary expert LLM assessment, aiming to improve confidence calibration in Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA). Experiments demonstrate that our method reduces the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) by an average of 40\% across three Medical VQA datasets, significantly enhancing MLLMs' reliability. The findings highlight the importance of domain-specific calibration for MLLMs in healthcare, offering a more trustworthy solution for AI-assisted diagnosis.

</details>


### [39] [SketchKeyAnime: Reference-anchored Sparse Key-Sketch Animation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19958)
*Meixi Li,Xianlin Zhang,Yue Zhang,Xueming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchKeyAnime 是一种基于视频扩散的动画生成框架，可在仅给定单张参考 RGB 图像和少量带时间索引的关键草图时，生成结构可控、外观一致且时序连贯的动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动画制作在关键姿态设计、中间帧生成和角色上色方面高度依赖人工；现有动画/视频生成方法通常需要 RGB 边界帧、密集逐帧条件或完整草图序列，输入成本较高，限制了低成本创作场景中的适用性。

Method: 论文提出双分支条件机制，同时编码局部几何约束与语义-时序上下文；设计 Sketch Cross Attention，通过可学习门控融合参考图像与草图条件；引入 Adaptive Weighted Loss，加强对关键草图帧和线稿区域的监督。

Result: 在 Sakuga-42M 的 Aesthetic 子集上，SketchKeyAnime 相比代表性动画插帧和草图引导生成基线表现更优；相较最佳基线，EDMD 降低 31.9%，FVD 降低 9.5%，并在多数定量指标上取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了稀疏关键草图条件下进行高保真、时序一致动画生成的可行性，展现了在低成本、高可控动画创作中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Traditional animation production relies heavily on manual drawing and iterative refinement, particularly for key-pose design, in-betweening, and character coloring. While existing animation and video generation methods have made notable progress, they typically depend on RGB boundary frames, dense frame-wise conditions, or complete sketch sequences, limiting their applicability under low-cost input conditions. We present SketchKeyAnime, a video diffusion framework for generating structurally controllable, appearance-consistent, and temporally coherent animations from sparse key-sketch inputs. Given a single reference RGB image and a few temporally indexed key sketches, SketchKeyAnime introduces a dual-branch conditioning mechanism to encode local geometric constraints alongside semantic-temporal context. It leverages Sketch Cross Attention to fuse reference image and sketch conditions with learnable gating, and incorporates an Adaptive Weighted Loss to strengthen supervision on key-sketch frames and line-art regions. Experimental results on the Aesthetic subset of Sakuga-42M show that our approach consistently outperforms representative animation interpolation and sketch-guided generation baselines. Compared to the best-performing baseline, SketchKeyAnime reduces EDMD by 31.9\% and FVD by 9.5\%, demonstrating superior sketch fidelity and temporal coherence, while achieving the best overall performance across most quantitative metrics. These results validate the proposed framework and highlight its potential for low-cost, highly controllable animation creation.

</details>


### [40] [Addressing Detail Bottlenecks in Latent Diffusion for RGB-to-SWIR Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19961)
*Kaili Wang,Martin Dimitrievski,Jose Maria Salvador,Ben Stoffelen,David Van Hamme,Lore Goetschalckx*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对潜扩散模型在图像到图像翻译中丢失细粒度空间信息的问题，提出 SCAE 与 LGE 两个轻量、骨干无关的改进模块，在 RGB-to-SWIR 驾驶场景翻译中显著提升下游检测性能并保持较优 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LDM 虽然高效，但压缩到潜空间时会损失空间细节；同时条件输入通常通过朴素下采样进入模型，进一步削弱源图像信号。这会影响依赖细节的下游感知任务，尤其是小目标检测。

Method: 作者提出 Source-Conditioned Autoencoder（SCAE），通过跳跃连接将高分辨率源图像特征注入解码器，以补偿自编码器压缩造成的信息损失；同时提出 Learnable Guidance Encoder（LGE），用可学习的条件编码替代朴素下采样。方法轻量且不依赖特定扩散骨干，并在 U-Net 与 DiT denoiser 上验证。

Result: 在 RGB-to-SWIR 驾驶场景翻译任务中，相比潜扩散基线，检测 mAP 最高提升约 2 倍；对 COCO-small 小目标提升最高达 3.4 倍；同时取得 state-of-the-art FID。作者还发现 FID 与检测性能相关性较弱，并在 RASMD 公共基准上实现 zero-shot 泛化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，仅优化生成质量指标不足以保证下游感知性能；通过改进自编码器与条件路径，可在保持生成质量的同时显著增强空间细节保真度和检测效果。论文强调应采用多维度评估生成式图像翻译模型。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) enable efficient image-to-image translation but discard fine spatial details during compression, degrading downstream perception tasks. We identify two bottlenecks: the autoencoder, which loses spatial information, and the conditioning pathway, which further degrades the source signal through naive downsampling. We propose two lightweight, backbone-agnostic fixes: a Source-Conditioned Autoencoder (SCAE) that injects high-resolution source features into the decoder via skip connections, and a Learnable Guidance Encoder (LGE) that replaces naive downsampling with a learned conditioning signal. Evaluated on RGB-to-SWIR translation for driving scenes with two denoiser backbones (U-Net and DiT), our approach improves detection mAP by up to 2x over the latent diffusion baseline, with up to 3.4x gains on small objects (COCO-small, <32^2 px^2), while achieving state-of-the-art FID. We further show that FID and detection performance are poorly correlated, motivating multi-axis evaluation. Results generalise zero-shot to the public RASMD benchmark. We will publicly release test data with annotations, all checkpoints, and training code.

</details>


### [41] [ROSE: Benchmarking the Perception-to-Action Gap in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19965)
*Yihao Wang,Zijian He,Jie Ren,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE基准，评估多模态大语言模型在相同视觉场景、不同任务上下文下，将视觉证据转化为对应动作的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM不仅需要理解图像，还需要根据任务语境采取不同动作；但同一视觉信息在不同上下文中可能要求不同输出，因此需要系统评估模型的上下文条件化视觉行动能力。

Method: 作者构建Reference-conditioned Oddity and Symbolic Execution（ROSE）控制基准，固定视觉场景，同时改变区域约束与符号输出要求；通过耦合的计数任务和坐标动作任务，测试模型能否推断隐式多数参照，并基于细粒度视觉证据执行上下文相关动作。

Result: 在九个近期MLLM上，模型从计数导向任务到区域条件动作任务的性能最高下降44.5个百分点，而人类表现达到98.8%。即使在同一模型能正确计数的配对场景和区域上，差距仍然存在；全局点击和匹配局部控制实验表明，坐标定位只能解释部分性能损失。

Conclusion: ROSE揭示了MLLM在将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作方面存在独立且模型相关的瓶颈，说明当前模型的视觉理解与任务条件化执行之间仍有显著脱节。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to act on visual information, yet the same scene may require different actions under different task contexts. How reliably can a model turn the same visual evidence into the action required by the current context? To answer this question, we introduce \textsc{ROSE} (\textbf{R}eference-conditioned \textbf{O}ddity and \textbf{S}ymbolic \textbf{E}xecution), a controlled benchmark that holds the visual scene fixed while varying region constraints and required symbolic outputs. Through coupled counting and coordinate-action tasks, \textsc{ROSE} tests whether models can infer an implicit majority reference and act on the resulting fine-grained visual evidence under changing contexts. Across nine recent MLLMs, performance drops by as much as 44.5 percentage points from counting-oriented tasks to region-conditioned action, despite 98.8\% human performance. The gap persists on paired scenes and regions for which the same model returns the correct count, while global-click and matched local controls show that coordinate grounding explains only part of the loss, revealing a distinct, model-dependent bottleneck in turning shared visual evidence into context-specific actions.

</details>


### [42] [Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion for Domain-Robust Whole-Slide Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19966)
*Yucheng Xing,Ling Huang,Pei Liu,Jingying Ma,Jiaqing Xu,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEFS框架，通过VQA从全切片图像中提取高层病理语义锚点，并与视觉证据进行不确定性-aware融合，以提升跨中心癌症生存预测的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI癌症预后方法多依赖像素级表征，容易受染色流程、扫描设备等中心特异性伪影影响，导致跨临床中心泛化能力不足。作者希望利用更接近病理医生诊断逻辑的高层语义信息，如肿瘤分级和微环境结构，构建更具域不变性的表示。

Method: 提出Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion Survival框架：首先利用视觉问答从WSI中获取语义锚点；其次采用双流WSI证据提取架构提取视觉证据；再使用基于Dirichlet分布的主观逻辑建模预测不确定性；最后通过谨慎合取规则融合语义证据与视觉证据，以避免相关信息源导致的过度自信。

Result: 模型仅在一个源域训练，并在四个未见目标域进行零样本评估，整体优于现有先进方法，在预测准确性和可靠性上均有提升，平均C-index提高10.2%。定量分析显示，VQA语义特征的跨中心差异显著低于像素特征。

Conclusion: 高层病理语义可作为更稳健的跨中心域不变表示；SAEFS通过语义锚定、证据不确定性建模与谨慎融合，有效提升了WSI生存预测在跨临床中心场景下的准确性、可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are widely used for computational cancer prognosis. However, most existing methods primarily focus on in-domain performance and fail to generalize across clinical centers. This limitation stems from their reliance on pixel-derived representations that are highly susceptible to domain-specific artifacts caused by staining protocols and scanner hardware. We hypothesize that high-level pathology semantics, such as tumor grade and micro-environmental architecture, provide a domain-invariant semantic representation that mirrors the robust diagnostic logic of human pathologists. Therefore, we propose a Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion Survival (SAEFS) framework, where SAEFS derives semantic anchors from WSIs via Visual Question Answering (VQA), employs a dual-stream WSI evidence extraction architecture, uses Dirichlet-based Subjective Logic to model uncertainty, and fuses semantic and visual evidence through a cautious conjunction rule to avoid overconfident fusion from correlated sources. Trained exclusively on one source domain and evaluated zero-shot across four unseen domains, SAEFS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models both in prediction accuracy and reliability, improving the average C-index by 10.2%. Quantitative analyses further show that VQA-derived semantic features exhibit significantly lower cross-center divergence than pixel-derived features, highlighting their robustness for cross-center clinical applications.

</details>


### [43] [CrossFlow: One-Step Generation Across Latent and Pixel Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19970)
*Xiyuan Wang,Xiao Zhang,Yang Li,Ruoxi Jiang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossFlow 提出跨空间流生成框架，从带噪潜变量直接生成像素图像，以减少潜空间生成目标与解码器输出质量之间的不匹配，并在 ImageNet-1k 256×256 上实现单步高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散/flow-matching 方法通常在同一表示空间中定义先验、概率路径和预测目标；潜扩散虽提升效率，但依赖单独训练的解码器，导致生成器优化目标在潜空间，而最终质量取决于解码器对生成潜变量的处理能力，存在训练与推理质量目标错位。

Method: 提出 CrossFlow：使用潜变量轨迹定义训练路径，但监督目标不是潜空间位移或速度，而是像素空间图像；核心为 velocity-free 的一步目标，使模型可直接从 noisy latent 映射到图像，同时可替代潜扩散管线中的解码器。训练中结合潜编码器以及像素空间感知损失、对抗损失以提升保真度。

Result: 在 class-conditional ImageNet-1k 256×256 任务上，CrossFlow-XL 仅需一次函数评估即可达到 1.62 FID；消融实验显示潜编码器、像素空间感知损失和对抗损失对图像保真度很关键。

Conclusion: 跨空间 flow 目标能够结合潜表示的计算效率与像素空间直接监督的质量优势，在推理时无需独立解码器，有望缓解潜扩散中潜空间优化与像素输出质量之间的错配问题。

Abstract: Most diffusion and flow-matching generators define the prior, probability path, and prediction target in the same representation space. Latent diffusion improves efficiency by moving this path into an autoencoder latent space, but the final sample is still produced by a separately trained decoder. This separation creates a mismatch: the generator is optimized for latent-space prediction, while final quality depends on how the decoder handles generated latents that may differ from clean encoder outputs. We introduce CrossFlow, a cross-space flow formulation that maps noisy latent inputs directly to pixel-space images. The key technical step is a velocity-free one-step objective: the latent trajectory defines the training path, but the supervised prediction is an image rather than a latent displacement. This lets one model act both as a one-step latent-to-pixel generator and as a decoder replacement for latent diffusion pipelines. On class-conditional ImageNet-1k at $256\times256$, CrossFlow-XL achieves 1.62 FID with one function evaluation. Ablations show that the latent encoder and pixel-space perceptual and adversarial losses are important for fidelity. These results indicate that cross-space flow objectives can combine the efficiency of latent representations with direct pixel-space supervision, without requiring a separate decoder at inference.

</details>


### [44] [Vision-Reasoning-Guided Occlusion Removal from Light Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19985)
*Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由视觉-语言推理引导的光场遮挡去除框架，将光场积分的物理可见性恢复能力与视觉语言模型的语义先验结合，用于在严重前景遮挡下恢复场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然环境中密集植被等前景遮挡会严重限制可见性，使遮挡鲁棒的场景恢复成为计算成像中的难题；单纯依赖物理成像或生成式语义补全都可能存在恢复不足或幻觉伪影。

Method: 首先利用多视角光场观测进行光场积分，抑制前景遮挡并生成初始可见性增强表示；随后引入视觉语言模型作为条件语义先验，在观测约束下恢复退化结构和细节；最后通过多样本融合聚合多个生成假设，以提升一致性并减少幻觉。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上取得先进性能；在四个合成光场基准场景（4-Syn）上获得最高平均 SSIM，并在结构化与非结构化采集设置中表现出较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结合物理成像约束与视觉语言推理能够提升严重遮挡条件下的鲁棒感知与场景恢复质量，适用于复杂自然环境中的探索性机器人导航等任务。

Abstract: Occlusion-robust scene recovery remains a major challenge in computational imaging, particularly in natural environments where dense foreground vegetation severely limits visibility. We propose a vision-reasoning-guided light field occlusion removal framework that combines the visibility recovery capability of light field integration (LFI) with the semantic reasoning capacity of vision-language models (VLMs). Multi-view observations are first integrated via LFI to suppress foreground occlusions and produce an initial visibility-enhanced representation. A VLM is then incorporated as a conditional semantic prior to restore degraded structures and recover fine details, guided by the observed measurements. To improve recovery consistency and reduce hallucination artifacts, we introduce a multi-sample fusion strategy that aggregates multiple generated hypotheses into a unified estimate. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, achieving the highest average SSIM across four synthetic light field benchmark scenes (4-Syn) and strong generalization across structured and unstructured acquisition settings. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining physical imaging constraints with vision-language reasoning for robust perception under severe occlusion, with applicability to search-and-rescue and exploratory robotic navigation.

</details>


### [45] [QG-MIL: A Gated Transformer Aggregator for Domain-Agnostic Multiple Instance Learning in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20027)
*Luca Zedda,Davide Antonio Mura,Cecilia Di Ruberto,Maurizio Atzori,Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Carsten Marr,Andrea Loddo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 QG-MIL，一种面向医学影像多实例学习的门控 Transformer 聚合器，通过稳定注意力分布提升预测鲁棒性，并在六个基准上取得优于主流方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的多实例学习聚合器在医学影像任务中容易出现注意力过度集中，导致模型预测过度自信、训练不稳定以及跨数据集泛化表现不一致。作者希望在不依赖额外损失、掩码或多阶段正则化的情况下缓解这一问题。

Method: QG-MIL 结合四个架构组件：基于 RMSNorm 的预归一化、逐头 QK 归一化、细粒度注意力输出门控，以及 SwiGLU 风格前馈模块。这些设计共同用于稳定 Transformer 聚合过程，使实例注意力分配更加均匀。

Result: 作者在六个医学影像 MIL 基准上评估模型，覆盖全切片病理图像与细胞级血液学两类不同规模任务。最佳 QG-MIL 变体在所有六个基准上超过领先基线，平均提升 +6.1 个 mean macro F1 点。注意力可视化和注意力质量分析显示其实例权重分布更分散；消融实验表明完整设计具有最稳定的跨域表现和更小方差。

Conclusion: QG-MIL 通过一组互补的 Transformer 稳定化与门控机制，有效缓解医学影像 MIL 中的注意力集中问题，提高了性能、稳定性和跨域一致性，具有较好的实用价值和可复现性。

Abstract: Attention-based Multiple Instance Learning aggregators in medical imaging are prone to attention concentration, producing overconfident and unstable predictions. We introduce QG-MIL, a gated transformer aggregator that addresses this through four synergistic architectural components: RMSNorm-based pre-normalization, per-head QK normalization, fine-grained attention output gating, and SwiGLU-style feed-forward modules. Together, these design choices stabilize training and distribute attention more uniformly across instances without auxiliary losses, masking, or multi-stage regularization. We evaluate QG-MIL across six benchmarks spanning whole-slide pathology and cell-level hematology, covering two fundamentally different MIL scales. The best-performing QG-MIL variants outperform leading baselines on all six benchmarks, with an average improvement of +6.1 mean macro F1 points. Attention overlays and attention mass analysis confirm more distributed instance weighting. Ablation studies show that while individual components can match the full model on specific datasets, the QG-MIL design provides the most consistent cross-domain performance and tightest variance when compared to selected baselines. We release a configurable implementation to support reproducibility at: https://github.com/unica-visual-intelligence-lab/QG-MIL

</details>


### [46] [ReA-OVCD: Reliability-Aware Open-Vocabulary Change Detection via Semantic and Spatial Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20032)
*Hongming Zhu,Huaji Chen,Bowen Du,Sicong Liu,Qin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的可靠性感知开放词汇变化检测框架 ReA-OVCD，可根据任意文本提示灵活检测遥感影像中的地表覆盖变化，并在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测方法在变化建模上存在权衡：实例级比较容易忽略局部细粒度语义变化，而像素级直接比较又因语义歧义和空间不一致产生不稳定响应与边界伪影，因此需要一种兼顾细粒度定位与可靠性的方案。

Method: 方法先利用像素级语义差异生成候选变化区域，以获得灵活且细致的定位；随后通过协同精炼策略从语义与空间两方面验证变化有效性。具体包括语义变化推理模块 SCR，联合分析分布差异与响应变化以抑制偶然不一致并保留可靠语义变化；以及边界感知变化精炼模块 BCR，通过检查候选区域是否由可靠内部像素支撑来缓解边界错位和不确定性。

Result: 在 LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、DSIFN 和 SECOND 多个数据集上的实验显示，该方法持续超过当前先进方法，F1^C 提升 2.13% 至 9.75%，并具有更高计算效率。

Conclusion: ReA-OVCD 通过可靠性感知的语义与边界协同精炼，有效解决开放词汇变化检测中的细粒度定位与可靠性矛盾，是一种高效、无需训练且泛化能力较强的遥感变化检测框架。

Abstract: Unlike traditional remote sensing change detection that relies on predefined categories, Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) identifies land cover changes flexibly using arbitrary text prompts. However, existing methods suffer from an inherent trade-off when modeling changes: instance-level comparison overlooks fine-grained semantic variations (e.g., partial building extensions), while direct pixel comparison proves unreliable, yielding unstable responses and boundary artifacts due to semantic ambiguity and spatial inconsistency. To this end, we propose an efficient training-free Reliability-Aware Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (ReA-OVCD) framework. It first derives candidate change regions from pixel-wise semantic discrepancies to ensure flexible and detailed localization. To ensure reliability, it subsequently introduces a collaborative refinement strategy to explicitly model change validity from both semantic and spatial perspectives. Specifically, we develop a Semantic Change Reasoning (SCR) module that reassesses changes by jointly analyzing distributional divergence and response variation, enabling the suppression of incidental inconsistencies while preserving reliable semantic shifts. In addition, a Boundary-aware Change Refinement (BCR) module is designed to mitigate artifacts stemming from boundary misalignment and uncertainty through validating whether candidate regions are supported by reliable interior pixels. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets (LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, DSIFN, and SECOND) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving $\mathrm{F}_{1}^{C}$ improvements of 2.13\% to 9.75\% with higher computational efficiency. The code is publicly available at \https://github.com/Funny0101/ReA-OVCD

</details>


### [47] [PU-UNet: Stable Multiplicative Interactions for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20035)
*Ziyuan Li,Osamah Sufyan,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PU-UNet，在 U-Net 的低分辨率阶段引入稳定的乘积单元残差块，以显式建模乘性特征交互，并在多个医学图像分割数据集上取得优于匹配 Residual U-Net 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集预测网络多依赖加性特征变换，高阶特征交互通常只被隐式建模；乘积单元虽可显式建模乘性关系，但传统对数-指数形式易产生数值不稳定，限制了其在深层分割网络中的应用。

Method: 提出 Product-Unit U-Net（PU-UNet），在残差 U-Net 架构中，将稳定化的 product-unit residual blocks 集成到信息丰富的低分辨率阶段；通过平滑正值映射与 log-domain clipping 结合，缓解数值不稳定，同时保持较低计算开销。

Result: 在 ISIC 2018、Kvasir-SEG 和 BUSI 数据集上分别取得 Dice 0.942、0.959 和最高 0.925；相比参数量、FLOPs 和推理延迟近似相同的 Residual U-Net，Dice 与 IoU 均稳定提升，并将 BUSI 正常样本的图像级假阳性率从 0.077 降至 0。消融实验显示收益来自 product-unit 交互，低分辨率放置效果最佳，稳定化设计有贡献。

Conclusion: 稳定的乘积单元残差学习能够以几乎不增加计算成本的方式，为 U-Net 类分割网络引入显式乘性特征交互，从而提升医学图像分割性能与部分场景下的误报控制能力。

Abstract: Many dense prediction networks rely on additive feature transformations and model higher-order feature interactions only implicitly. Product units provide an explicit mechanism for multiplicative feature modeling, but their logarithmic--exponential formulation can cause numerical instability, which has limited their use in deep dense prediction networks. In this work, we propose Product-Unit U-Net (PU-UNet), a residual U-Net that integrates stable product-unit residual blocks into rich low-resolution stages for medical image segmentation. The proposed formulation combines smooth positivity mapping with log-domain clipping, enabling stable multiplicative feature learning with negligible computational overhead. On ISIC 2018, Kvasir-SEG, and BUSI, PU-UNet achieves Dice scores of 0.942, 0.959, and up to 0.925, respectively. Compared with a matched Residual U-Net baseline, PU-UNet consistently improves Dice and IoU while keeping parameters, FLOPs, and inference latency nearly unchanged, and reduces the image-level false-positive rate on normal BUSI cases from 0.077 to zero. Ablation studies suggest that the gains are associated with product-unit interactions, are strongest under low-resolution placement, and benefit from the proposed stabilization design. These results suggest that stable product-unit residual learning can be an effective way to enhance U-Net-style segmentation networks with explicit multiplicative interactions.

</details>


### [48] [FUSE: Frequency-domain Unification and Spectral Energy Alignment for Multi-modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20044)
*Xuanhao Qi,Tom H. Luan,Yukang Zhang,Jinkai Zheng,Zhou Su,Shuwei Li,Lei Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FUSE，将多模态 ReID 从频域视角建模，通过频谱解耦与能量对齐提升跨模态身份表征的完整性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 ReID 方法过度依赖低频线索，如颜色、光照和粗粒度外观，忽略中高频中的几何结构、纹理和身份判别细节，导致频谱表征不完整、跨模态对齐不稳定。

Method: 方法包含两阶段：首先用 Spectral Decomposition Module（SDM）自适应地将特征划分为低频、中频和高频子空间，进行层次化频谱建模；随后用 Cross-Modal Alignment Module（CAM）通过频率一致性正则化约束跨模态能量对齐与子空间互补性，并引入可学习频率调制以增强对光照变化和异构传感器条件的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 RGBNT201、RGBNT100 和 MSVR310 数据集上进行实验，FUSE 相比已有方法取得 9.1% mAP 和 9.5% Rank-1 的提升。

Conclusion: FUSE 证明了频域建模可有效补足多模态 ReID 中被忽视的中高频判别信息，并通过频谱解耦与跨模态能量对齐提升表示学习的可解释性、鲁棒性和检索性能。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in multi-modal Re-Identification (ReID), existing methods tend to emphasize low-frequency cues. Consequently, they focus on attributes such as color, illumination, and coarse appearance, while overlooking mid and high-frequency structures that encode geometric, textural, and identity-discriminative details. This imbalance leads to incomplete spectral representations and unstable cross-modal alignment. To overcome these limitations, we introduce FUSE, a frequency-domain framework that reformulates multi-modal ReID as a two-stage process of spectral disentanglement and energy alignment. The proposed Spectral Decomposition Module (SDM) adaptively partitions features into low, mid, and high-frequency subspaces, enabling hierarchical spectral modeling. The Cross-Modal Alignment Module (CAM) further enforces energy alignment and subspace complementarity across modalities via frequency-consistency regularization. In addition, FUSE incorporates learnable frequency modulation to enhance robustness under varying illumination and heterogeneous sensor conditions. Extensive experiments on RGBNT201, RGBNT100, and MSVR310 show that FUSE achieves 9.1\% mAP and 9.5\% Rank-1 improvements, establishing an interpretable frequency-domain paradigm for multi-modal representation learning.

</details>


### [49] [See-and-Reach: Precise Vision-Language Navigation for UAVs within the Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20045)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Yantian Shen,Zhikun Hu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 UAV-VLN-FOV 任务与 3DG-VLN 框架，用于诊断并提升无人机在目标已进入视野后的精准“看见并到达”能力，实验显示成功率较强基线提升 13.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UAV 视觉-语言导航通常将远距离搜索目标与最终接近目标联合建模和评估，难以单独衡量无人机在目标可见后，能否准确进行视觉 grounding，并将视觉-语言证据转化为精确三维运动的终端到达能力。

Method: 作者引入 UAV-VLN-FOV 目标可见导航任务，隔离 see-and-reach 阶段；提出 3DG-VLN 视觉-语言航点预测框架，利用动态三维方向线索指导导航。方法自适应处理高分辨率前视与下视观测以保留细粒度视觉和几何信息，并在闭环导航中在线更新目标相对方向，减少方向漂移。

Result: 构建了包含 2,717 条轨迹的高分辨率基准数据集，包含面向目标的高层语言指令、高分辨率前视/下视第一视角观测以及连续三维航点标注。实验表明 3DG-VLN 优于竞争性 UAV-VLN 基线，成功率提升 13.82%；真实世界试验也显示其实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作将 UAV-VLN 中的终端目标到达能力作为独立任务进行诊断评估，并通过动态 3D 方向引导与高分辨率多视角观测提升目标 grounding 和空间对齐能力，为精确无人机 see-and-reach 导航提供了有效框架和基准。

Abstract: UAV Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) is typically formulated as a holistic search-and-reach problem, where long-range target discovery and final target approach are optimized and evaluated jointly. This formulation makes it difficult to assess a critical capability of aerial embodied agents, namely whether a UAV can accurately ground a visible target and translate vision-language evidence into precise 3D motion once the target enters its field of view. To address this limitation, we introduce UAV-VLN-FOV, a target-visible navigation task that isolates the see-and-reach stage and enables a more diagnostic evaluation of terminal reaching ability. We further propose 3DG-VLN, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework guided by dynamic 3D direction cues to enhance fine-grained visual grounding and spatial direction alignment for precise target reaching. Specifically, 3DG-VLN adaptively processes high-resolution front-view and downward-view observations to preserve fine-grained visual and geometric details for target grounding. It also updates the target-relative direction online during closed-loop navigation, allowing the agent to maintain spatial alignment with the target and reduce accumulated direction drift. To support this task, we construct a dedicated high-resolution benchmark which contains 2,717 trajectories with target-oriented high-level instructions, high-resolution front-view and downward-view egocentric observations, and continuous 3D waypoint annotations. Experiments show that 3DG-VLN outperforms competitive UAV-VLN baselines, achieving a 13.82\% improvement in success rate. Real-world trials further demonstrate the potential of 3DG-VLN for practical see-and-reach navigation. The source code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/xuefanfu/3DG-VLN.

</details>


### [50] [Variable-Length Tokenization via Learnable Global Merging for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20076)
*Dong Hoon Lee,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于 token 合并的可变长度 tokenizer，用于缓解传统截断式 VLT 在不同长度间的表示不对齐问题，从而提升 LDM/DiT 的质量-计算量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型的质量与计算成本受固定压缩率 tokenizer 限制；可变长度 tokenizer 虽能自适应 token 数量，但传统按序截断会导致 token 语义依赖位置，引发跨长度潜在分布偏移，使单一可变长度扩散模型难以稳定工作。

Method: 作者提出通过合并 token 而非截断 token 来调节序列长度，并鼓励相似 token 合并，使扩散 Transformer 可依据合并模式实现跨长度表示对齐。为避免传统数据依赖合并在生成阶段不可获得的问题，进一步提出数据无关的可学习全局合并机制。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 生成任务上，该合并式可变长度 tokenizer 与扩散 Transformer 结合，相比既有 VLT 方法取得更优的 gFID 与计算成本权衡。

Conclusion: 基于合并的可变长度 tokenization 能有效缓解跨长度表示错位与分布偏移问题，为潜在扩散模型提供更灵活且高效的质量-计算控制方案。

Abstract: Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have become dominant in visual synthesis, but their quality-compute trade-off is largely constrained by the tokenizer's fixed compression ratio. Variable-length tokenizers (VLTs) promise adaptive compression by varying token counts, allowing diffusion models to flexibly balance quality and compute. However, conventional VLTs modulate length by truncating ordered token sequences, which makes token semantics depend on token position and breaks representational alignment across lengths. This leads to a cross-length shift in the latent distribution that hinders a single variable-length diffusion model from operating effectively. To address this, we propose a novel variable-length tokenizer that modulates length by merging tokens. We show that encouraging similar tokens to merge enables direct cross-length representation alignment when the diffusion transformer operates according to the merging pattern. Since conventional merging methods are data-dependent, making the merging pattern inaccessible during generation, we introduce learnable global merging, which is data-independent, to ensure compatibility with diffusion transformers. On ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, our merging-based variable-length tokenizer integrated with a diffusion transformer achieves a superior gFID-compute trade-off compared to prior VLT methods. Code is available at [this https URL](https://github.com/movinghoon/lgm)

</details>


### [51] [The Hidden Evolution of Disguised Visual Context inside the VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20077)
*Wish Suharitdamrong,Tony Alex,Muhammad Awais,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较视觉语言模型中两类视觉信息接入LLM的架构：将视觉token作为输入序列中的上下文提示，或注入到LLM中间层；研究发现不同接入方式会导致视觉表征在LLM内部以不同方式演化，并影响下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将视觉token接入LLM的方式多样，但视觉token如何从原始视觉信号转化为可与语言空间交互的表征，以及不同集成架构如何影响这一过程，仍缺乏受控、公平的系统比较。

Method: 在相同训练条件下，对in-context输入序列注入与layer-wise中间层注入两种VLM集成范式进行公平评估，覆盖单图、多图和视频基准；同时分析视觉token在LLM各层中的表征演化、频率特征、语言空间对齐和注意力分配。

Result: 视觉token最初以缺乏语言结构的原始视觉上下文形式进入LLM，随后会随集成范式不同而被逐步重塑；两种范式捕获视觉信号的频率特征不同，进而影响可利用的视觉特征、视觉-语言对齐质量和不同任务上的性能。研究还表明，仅靠注意力分配无法解释性能差异。

Conclusion: VLM性能主要取决于各层视觉表征质量及其与语言空间的有效融合，而非简单的注意力强弱；不同视觉注入架构会塑造截然不同的内部视觉表征演化路径，因此架构选择对模型能力具有根本影响。

Abstract: Visual tokens enter Large Language Models (LLMs) as raw, foreign signals. How they are transformed into meaningful representations and interact with the language space depends entirely on the integration architecture. Whether by treating visual tokens as in-context prompts within the input sequence or injecting them directly into the LLM's intermediate layers. A controlled comparison and understanding of how these architectural choices affect visual information and its internal transformation to integrate with the LLM remains underexplored. We provide a fair comparison by evaluating in-context and layer-wise injection VLM integration paradigms under identical training conditions across single image, multi-image, and video benchmarks. In doing so, we uncover a hidden evolution where visual tokens enter the LLM as disguised visual context, raw representations lacking linguistic structure, but are progressively reshaped depending on the integration paradigm, each capturing fundamentally different frequency characteristics of the visual signal. We show that this evolution inside the LLM determines what visual features the VLM can utilize effectively, how visual representations align with the language space, and ultimately how each paradigm performs across different tasks. We further demonstrate that attention allocation alone is insufficient, and that performance is driven by the quality of visual representations at each layer.

</details>


### [52] [Holo-World: Unified Camera, Object and Weather Control for Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20083)
*Xiangchen Yin,Wenzhang Sun,Jiahui Yuan,Zijie Liu,Yinda Chen,Wei Li,Dachun Kai,Chunfeng Wang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Holo-World：一种从单张首帧出发、同时支持相机运动、物体运动与天气状态控制的视频世界模型，并构建 HoloStateData 数据集用于统一监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频世界模型通常将相机控制、物体控制和天气/环境状态生成分开处理；天气生成往往依赖已有源视频或重建场景，未来结构已被输入隐式指定。作者希望研究更具挑战性的“首帧锚定 source-to-state”设定：仅给定单张图像，结合显式相机/物体控制和可选天气指令，生成保持源世界或迁移到目标天气状态的视频。

Method: 作者构建 HoloStateData，将多样视频转化为统一的相机、物体和天气控制样本；提出 Holo-World 统一可控视频世界模型。核心包括 Unified Scene Adapter，将世界保持与天气迁移分解到不同参数子空间，并利用渲染背景、几何缓冲和物体控制维持场景结构，同时建模天气相关外观和粒子效果；此外提出 Scene-Weather Decomposed CFG，分别引导场景残差和天气残差，以增强目标天气效果并避免整体条件过度放大。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，Holo-World 能在保持一致场景结构的同时，实现精确的相机与物体控制，并将场景迁移到多种目标天气状态；在天气状态生成任务上优于视频到视频天气编辑基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将单图像锚定的视频世界建模扩展到统一的运动控制与环境状态迁移，为可控视频生成提供了更完整的框架，尤其适合需要同时保持场景结构、控制动态元素并改变天气状态的应用。

Abstract: Video world models are moving toward preserving an observed world under controllable camera and object motion while allowing its environmental state to change. Yet these controls remain isolated, and weather generation typically relies on a source video or reconstructed scene that already specifies future structure. We study a first-frame-anchored source-to-state setting, where the model starts from a single image and follows explicit camera and object controls and an optional weather instruction, then generates a video that either preserves the source world or transfers it to a target weather state. To address these challenges, we first build HoloStateData, a state video dataset that turns diverse videos into unified control samples for camera, object, and weather supervision. Second, we introduce Holo-World, a unified controllable video world model that jointly controls scene from a single image. Its Unified Scene Adapter factorizes world preservation and weather transfer into distinct parameter subspaces, using rendered background, geometry buffers, and object controls to maintain controlled scene structure while modeling weather-dependent appearance and particle effects. Additionally, Scene-Weather Decomposed CFG guides scene and weather residuals separately, strengthening target weather effects without over-amplifying the full condition. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that Holo-World maintains precise camera and object control with consistent scene structure while transferring scenes into diverse target weather state, outperforming video-to-video weather editing baselines on weather-state generation. Our project page is available at \url{https://xiangchenyin.github.io/Holo-World/}.

</details>


### [53] [EventVLA: Event-Driven Visual Evidence Memory for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20092)
*Ganlin Yang,Zhangzheng Tu,Yuqiang Yang,Sitong Mao,Junyi Dong,Tianxing Chen,Jiaqi Peng,Jing Xiong,Jiafei Cao,Jifeng Dai,Wengang Zhou,Yao Mu,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EventVLA，通过稀疏视觉证据记忆缓解长时程机器人操作中的记忆瓶颈，并在仿真与真实双臂任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作中，任务相关视觉线索可能随时间被遮挡或不可见；现有 VLA 记忆方法存在信息瓶颈、高延迟或无选择缓存导致冗余严重等问题。

Method: 构建端到端 EventVLA 框架，包含基础视觉锚点用于保留初始与短期上下文，以及动态 Keyframe Evidence Memory（KEM）模块。KEM 从 VLA 潜在嵌入中预测未来关键帧概率，选择性捕获并存储任务关键视觉事件。

Result: 在 17 个需要记忆的仿真任务和 4 个真实双臂任务中，EventVLA 相比最先进的记忆增强 VLA 平均成功率提升 40%。

Conclusion: 稀疏、前瞻性、任务相关的视觉证据记忆能够有效改善非马尔可夫机器人操作任务中的长期依赖建模，并提升 VLA 策略性能。

Abstract: Memory remains a critical bottleneck for long-horizon robotic manipulation, as standard Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often fail when task-relevant cues become occluded or unobservable over time. While existing memory-augmented methods utilize historical context, they either suffer from severe information bottlenecks, incur high latency via decoupled dual systems, or rely on unselective buffers that accumulate massive visual redundancies. To address these limitations, we introduce EventVLA, an end-to-end framework founded on the concept of sparse visual evidence memory that comprises two core components: foundational visual anchors to retain initial and short-term contexts, and a dynamic Keyframe Evidence Memory (KEM) module. Specifically, KEM directly predicts future keyframe probabilities from the VLA's latent embeddings to autonomously capture and store sparse, task-critical visual events. This foresight-driven mechanism empowers the policy to dynamically evaluate the future causal utility of current observations, preserving transient visual evidence before it becomes unobservable. Furthermore, we propose RoboTwin-MeM, a diagnostic benchmark specifically designed to evaluate non-Markovian manipulation tasks with interactive visual evidence. Extensive evaluations show that across 17 memory-requiring simulation tasks and 4 real-world bimanual tasks, EventVLA achieves an average success rate improvement of +40% over state-of-the-art memory-augmented VLAs.

</details>


### [54] [MakeupMirror: Improving Facial Attribute Preservation in Diffusion Models for Makeup Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20094)
*Nefeli Andreou,Angel Martínez-González,Sabine Sternig,Matthieu Guillaumin,Epameinondas Antonakos,Michael Opitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MakeupMirror 是一种面向妆容迁移与虚拟试妆的扩散式方法，重点改进身份特征与肤色保持，并在质量与速度上优于 Stable-Makeup。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式妆容迁移方法虽能提升真实感与准确性，但在跨主体迁移时仍容易改变用户身份特征和肤色，限制了其在生产级线上虚拟试妆中的应用。

Method: 该方法在 Stable-Makeup 基础上引入面部几何条件控制的 ControlNets、分区域妆容迁移控制、基于肤色的迁移调制机制，以及 Levenberg-Marquardt Langevin 采样器，以同时提升面部保真、区域控制、肤色保持和推理速度。

Result: 在 CPM-Real、Makeup Wild 和新收集的 MakeupSelfies 数据集上，MakeupMirror 相比 Stable-Makeup 将相对人脸识别相似度提升 60%，相对肤色差异降低 50%，推理延迟为 0.7 秒，并在核心身份保持标准上达到 94% 的专家接受率。

Conclusion: MakeupMirror 显著推进了扩散式妆容迁移在身份保持、肤色一致性、区域可控性和实时性方面的能力，更接近可用于线上美妆购物的生产级虚拟试妆系统。

Abstract: Makeup transfer models enable fun augmented reality (AR) experiences as well as virtual try-on (VTO) for online makeup shopping. While recent state-of-the-art diffusion based solutions such as Stable-Makeup dramatically improve the accuracy and realism of makeup transfer, they still face limitations in identity and skin color preservation, making production-level VTO for makeup shopping unrealistic. In this work, we propose MakeupMirror, a diffusion-based approach to makeup transfer that makes significant progress towards preserving facial features and skin tone. We introduce several technical innovations over Stable-Makeup: (1) integration of facial geometry conditioning with ControlNets to maintain facial fidelity; (2) region-specific makeup transfer control to enable precise makeup application across facial regions such as skin, eyes and lips; (3) skin tone-based makeup transfer modulation that prevent skin tone alteration in cross-subject transfer scenarios; and (4) integration of a Levenberg-Marquardt Langevin sampler to speed up inference while maintaining generation quality. Our experiments on CPM-Real, Makeup Wild, and (herein newly collected, more diverse) MakeupSelfies datasets show that MakeupMirror improves relative facial recognition similarity by +60%, reduces relative skin tone difference by -50% over Stable-Makeup, with a latency of 0.7s, while achieving expert acceptance rate of 94% across core facial identity preservation criteria.

</details>


### [55] [Stitching and dimensionality effects on large artificially generated volume datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20095)
*Lucas von Chamier,Jan Philipp Albrecht,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究大图像生成中分块推理/拼接产生的伪影对基于 CycleGAN 的冷冻电镜风格迁移及下游线粒体分割的影响，指出感知指标不足以评估真实任务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺寸科学图像受显存限制通常需要切块处理再拼接输出，但相邻块边界不一致会产生拼接伪影。此类伪影对分割任务已有影响认知，但对风格迁移类生成模型，尤其在生物医学图像域适应中的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 作者在冷冻电镜数据集上训练 CycleGAN，比较三种拼接策略与两类 patch 维度设置（2D 与 3D）。评估包括感知质量指标（如 FID）以及下游线粒体分割性能，并进一步测试从三个正交方向集成预测对不同质量体数据的作用。

Result: 结果显示：FID 无法检测会显著损害下游分割性能的细微拼接伪影；采用无伪影拼接的 3D 模型在下游任务上略优于 2D 模型，但收益很小，难以抵消计算成本；2D 模型因可使用更大 batch size，训练更稳定；三正交方向集成可改善低质量体数据，但对高质量输出无明显收益。

Conclusion: 在大规模生物医学科学图像生成与域适应中，仅优化感知指标并不充分，必须显式考虑并缓解拼接伪影；2D/3D 模型选择应在性能增益、稳定性和计算成本之间权衡。

Abstract: Generating large images via deep learning requires patching input data to accommodate hardware memory limitations, then assembling output patches, a process that can introduce stitching artifacts when neighboring patches do not align at borders. While these artifacts are known to affect segmentation tasks, their impact on generative models for style-transfer remains poorly understood. We investigated three stitching approaches and two patch dimensionalities (2D vs 3D) using cycleGAN models trained on cryo-electron microscopy datasets. We evaluated both perceptual quality and performance on downstream mitochondria segmentation. Our key findings reveal that: (1) FID scores fail to detect subtle stitching artifacts that significantly impact downstream segmentation performance, (2) 3D models with artifact-free stitching marginally outperform 2D models on downstream tasks, though the improvement barely justifies the computational cost, and (3) 2D models train more stably due to larger batch sizes. Additionally, we demonstrate that ensembling predictions from three orthogonal directions can improve low-quality volumes but provides no benefit for high-quality outputs. These results demonstrate that maximizing generative model performance on large scientific datasets requires careful consideration and mitigation of stitching artifacts, and that perceptual metrics alone are insufficient for evaluating domain adaptation quality in biomedical imaging.

</details>


### [56] [WeGenBench: A Multidimensional Diagnostic Benchmark towards Text-to-Image Model Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20100)
*Qian Liang,Xiaomin Li,Ying Zhang,Jia Xu,Lihao Ni,Hongrui Li,Jingjing Li,Jing Lyu,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WeGenBench，一个用于文本到图像生成模型的双语、多维度综合评测基准，包含 4,000 条中英文提示词，并结合场景分类、多维标签与基于 VLM 的新指标来定位模型在具体生成类别中的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成评测基准难以从多个维度全面、准确地衡量模型能力，尤其不易揭示模型在特定场景、语言或细粒度内容类别上的缺陷。因此需要一个覆盖中英文、支持跨维度分析且能提供可解释评测依据的新基准。

Method: 作者构建了包含 4,000 个测试提示词的 WeGenBench，按中文和英文进行均衡设计，并在宏观场景分类之外，为每条提示词标注多维标签。评测机制结合场景分类与标签维度进行交叉分析，同时引入视觉语言模型设计并验证新的评测指标，从三个核心方面评估生成质量，并输出评测结果及推理轨迹。

Result: 作者使用 WeGenBench 对当前先进文本到图像生成方法进行了系统评测，展示该基准能够更精确地发现模型在特定语言、场景和细粒度生成任务中的短板，并提供更具可验证性的评测依据。

Conclusion: WeGenBench 为文本到图像生成模型提供了更全面、细粒度且可解释的评测框架，有助于推动模型在双语理解、复杂场景生成和领域特定任务上的改进。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image generation models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing highly realistic images from text inputs alone. Although existing benchmarks can evaluate the generation capabilities of various models to some extent, they struggle to comprehensively and accurately measure performance across multiple dimensions, often failing to reveal the inherent deficiencies of models in specific categories. To address these limitations, we propose WeGenBench, a novel benchmark designed for the comprehensive, multi-perspective evaluation of text-to-image generation capabilities. Our benchmark comprises a total of 4,000 test prompts across two primary categories, meticulously balanced between Chinese and English to evaluate bilingual and cross-cultural generation capabilities. Beyond macroscopic scene classification, we annotate each prompt with multi-dimensional tags tailored to the distinct content and challenges of each language, thereby refining the generation tasks into more specific sub-categories. Through a cross-dimensional evaluation mechanism leveraging both scene classifications and multi-dimensional tags, WeGenBench can precisely pinpoint model shortcomings in specific generation categories. Furthermore, to measure generation quality more accurately, we design and validate several novel evaluation metrics by integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which assess model performance on domain-specific tasks from three core aspects. Crucially, our approach yields both the assessment outcomes and the detailed reasoning trajectories, facilitating a rigorous verification of the accuracy and soundness of the evaluation results. Finally, we conduct systematic benchmarking on current state-of-the-art methods and provide an in-depth analysis of the limitations present in existing models.

</details>


### [57] [Geometry-Preserving in 3D Gaussian Splatting for LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20103)
*Kyoleen Kwak,Daeho Kim,Jeong Woon Lee,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无靶标 LiDAR-相机外参标定的 3DGS 框架，通过保持高斯代理的度量几何来提升标定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无靶标标定方法缺少足够判别性的跨模态特征；基于可微重建和 3D Gaussian Splatting 的方法虽能利用密集光度监督优化外参，但往往过度追求渲染质量，使高斯代理几何偏离真实 LiDAR 结构，影响标定准确性。

Method: 方法聚合多视角 LiDAR 观测以提供密集深度监督，并阻断光度梯度对高斯空间参数的更新，从而防止光度优化破坏由 LiDAR 约束得到的度量几何；随后在该几何保持的可微框架中优化 LiDAR-相机外参。

Result: 在公开自动驾驶数据集上进行验证，结果显示该方法在标定精度上稳定优于现有无靶标方法。

Conclusion: 保持 3DGS 代理与真实 LiDAR 几何的一致性，比单纯提升渲染质量更有利于无靶标 LiDAR-相机标定；所提出的深度监督与梯度阻断策略能有效提升外参估计可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate LiDAR-camera calibration is essential for robust multi-modal perception. Targetless approaches avoid manual setup but remain limited by the scarcity of discriminative cross-modal features. Recent methods address this by reconstructing the scene within a differentiable model, enabling extrinsic optimization through dense photometric supervision. Among these, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has been widely adopted as a geometric proxy that bridges LiDAR and camera within a single differentiable framework. However, since 3DGS was originally designed for novel view synthesis, existing methods tend to prioritize rendering quality, causing the proxy geometry to drift from the true LiDAR structure. We propose a framework that preserves the metric geometry of the Gaussian proxy by aggregating multi-view LiDAR observations for dense depth supervision and blocking photometric gradients from updating the Gaussian spatial parameters. We validate our method on public driving datasets, where it consistently outperforms existing targetless methods in calibration accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [EFIQA: Explainable Fundus Image Quality Assessment via Anatomical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20108)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Qian Wan,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EFIQA是一种无需质量标签监督的眼底图像质量评估框架，可生成空间质量图，并在外部数据集上较监督方法表现更好且更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习图像质量评估方法依赖特定数据集的质量标签，导致泛化能力受标签标准限制；同时通常只输出整体质量判断，无法定位图像质量退化区域，缺乏空间可解释性。

Method: 提出EFIQA框架，不学习人工标注的“退化是什么”，而利用解剖先验学习“正常应存在什么”。在眼底摄影任务中，采用两阶段流程：首先通过掩码解剖结构修复训练无监督异常检测器，用于发现血管缺失等异常区域；随后将该先验知识蒸馏到一个浅层适配器中，把冻结基础模型的特征映射为精细质量图。

Result: 在外部数据集评估中，该无标签方法仅需最小适配，就能在不同质量判定标准的基准上优于监督式方法，并提供更好的空间可解释性。

Conclusion: EFIQA展示了利用解剖先验进行无监督图像质量评估的可行性，能够缓解标签依赖与泛化受限问题，并通过质量图提升临床或实际应用中的可解释性与可用性。

Abstract: Image quality control is vital for a wide range of downstream applications. Deep learning-based image quality assessment methods typically train classifiers on dataset-specific quality labels, inheriting two limitations: (1) generalization is tied to the labeling criteria of the training set and (2) these methods cannot provide spatial feedback on where the quality is degraded, lacking explainability. In this work, we propose EFIQA, a framework that requires no quality-related supervision and produces spatial quality maps by design. Rather than learning ``what is degradation" from human-annotated labels, EFIQA learns ``what should be there" by leveraging anatomical priors. For fundus photography, we instantiate this as a two-stage approach, by first training an unsupervised anomaly detector via masked anatomical inpainting to identify regions of missing vasculature, and then distilling this prior knowledge into a shallow adapter mapping features of a frozen foundation model to precise quality maps. External-dataset evaluation demonstrates that this label-free approach with minimal adaptation achieves better performance and explainability compared with supervised methods across benchmarks with different quality criteria, highlighting its potential for real-world applications.

</details>


### [59] [FrozenDrive: Zero-Shot Text-Guided Driving Scene Generation and Data Augmentation with Parameter-Free Frozen Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20110)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Hyeonseong Kim,Daehyun We,Seonkyu Song,Jinnyeong Yang,Hyun-Kurl Jang,Youngho Yoon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrozenDrive 是一种用于自动驾驶合成数据生成的可控扩散框架，在冻结预训练扩散模型参数的前提下，实现多视角与时序一致的驾驶场景生成，并提升罕见类别和恶劣条件下的数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的自动驾驶合成数据方法通常需要微调骨干网络或添加额外层来保证多视角、时序一致性，这会损害预训练知识和文本对齐能力；同时模型容易受训练分布限制，在恶劣天气、未见配置和罕见类别上表现不足。

Method: FrozenDrive 使用驾驶栈信号与文本提示进行条件控制，引入知识保持的时空注意力机制，在参数冻结的扩散骨干中以单次生成方式实现跨视角对齐和时间一致性；同时加入面向目标的约束以提升单个物体，尤其是罕见类别的生成保真度。

Result: 在无需针对天气或场景进行专门微调的情况下，FrozenDrive 能生成全局一致的多视角驾驶场景，尤其适用于恶劣天气与罕见条件；在 nuScenes 上优于既有基线，并且用其增强数据训练自动驾驶模型可显著提升性能，特别是在夜间和雨天场景中。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在保持预训练扩散模型知识不被破坏的同时，通过参数无关的时空一致性建模与目标级约束，可以有效生成高质量、可控且面向长尾场景的自动驾驶合成数据，从而增强下游自动驾驶模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic data for autonomous driving is surging, powered by diffusion models that promise scalable scene generation. Yet key obstacles remain, as enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency often relies on backbone fine-tuning or added layers, which erodes pre-trained knowledge and weakens text alignment. Models also stay close to the training distribution, struggling under adverse weather and unseen configurations, and fidelity favors frequent over rare classes. We address these gaps with FrozenDrive, a controllable generative framework that preserves a pretrained diffusion models knowledge while achieving strong consistency. FrozenDrive conditions on rich driving-stack signals and text prompts, and introduces knowledge-preserving spatio-temporal attention to impose cross-view alignment and temporal coherence in a single pass within a parameter-free frozen diffusion backbone. An additional object-focused constraint improves per-object fidelity for rare categories. Without any weather- or scene-specific fine-tuning, our model synthesizes globally coherent multi-view driving scenes from text, particularly under adverse and rare conditions, and surpasses prior baselines. On nuScenes, FrozenDrive augmented data significantly improves AD models performance, especially at night and in rain, demonstrating stronger robustness when trained with our scenario-targeted data.

</details>


### [60] [Pixel-Level Residual Diffusion Transformer: Scalable 3D CT Volume Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20112)
*Zhenkai Zhang,Markus Hiller,Krista A. Ehinger,Tom Drummond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRDiT 是一种用于直接生成高分辨率 3D CT 体数据的像素/体素级残差扩散 Transformer 框架，通过局部低频建模与全局高频残差建模提升生成质量与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 医学影像生成模型在生成高分辨率、细节丰富的 CT 体数据时面临计算开销大、优化困难、细微结构保真不足以及自编码器瓶颈带来的信息损失等问题。

Method: 提出两阶段训练架构：第一阶段使用基于 MLP 的局部盲估计去噪器，在重叠 3D patch 上高效分离低频结构；第二阶段使用带内存高效注意力机制的全局残差扩散 Transformer，对整幅体数据的高频残差进行建模与细化，形成由粗到细的体素级生成流程。

Result: 在 LIDC-IDRI 和 RAD-ChestCT 数据集上的实验表明，PRDiT 相比 HA-GAN、3D LDM、WDM-3D 等方法取得更低的 3D FID、MMD 和 Wasserstein distance，显示出更好的生成质量与分布匹配能力。

Conclusion: PRDiT 通过低频结构与高频残差解耦建模，降低了优化难度并增强训练稳定性，同时避免自编码器压缩瓶颈，更适合高分辨率 3D CT 体数据的高保真生成。

Abstract: Generating high-resolution 3D CT volumes with fine details remains challenging due to substantial computational demands and optimization difficulties inherent to existing generative models. In this paper, we propose the Pixel-Level Residual Diffusion Transformer (PRDiT), a scalable generative framework that synthesizes high-quality 3D medical volumes directly at voxel-level. PRDiT introduces a two-stage training architecture comprising 1) a local denoiser in the form of an MLP-based blind estimator operating on overlapping 3D patches to separate low-frequency structures efficiently, and 2) a global residual diffusion transformer employing memory-efficient attention to model and refine high-frequency residuals across entire volumes. This coarse-to-fine modeling strategy simplifies optimization, enhances training stability, and effectively preserves subtle structures without the limitations of an autoencoder bottleneck. Extensive experiments conducted on the LIDC-IDRI and RAD-ChestCT datasets demonstrate that PRDiT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, such as HA-GAN, 3D LDM and WDM-3D, achieving significantly lower 3D FID, MMD and Wasserstein distance scores.

</details>


### [61] [SAM3 Self-Distillation for Fine-Grained GOOSE 2D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20130)
*Xuesong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍了 ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D 细粒度语义分割挑战赛第4名方案，基于 SAM3 图像编码器与轻量解码器，在官方测试集上达到 69.73% composite mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向细粒度语义分割任务，作者希望利用视觉基础模型 SAM3 的强表征能力，同时通过轻量化适配与推理/训练策略提升固定输入尺寸模型在挑战赛数据上的性能。

Method: 方法包括：将 SAM3 的图像编码器适配到分割框架并配备轻量解码器；使用由 SAM3 在真实框提示下生成的结果作为教师，在其优于学生模型的类别上进行自蒸馏；提出图像级多尺度测试时增强，通过缩放原图而非模型输入恢复多尺度推理；引入强光度扰动数据增强。

Result: 该方案在 1,815 张官方测试图像上取得 69.73% composite mIoU，获得挑战赛第4名。实验发现，来自 2025 年优胜方案的强光度扰动是该流水线中最大的单项性能提升来源。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，视觉基础模型编码器结合轻量解码器能够有效迁移到细粒度语义分割；类别选择性的 SAM3 自蒸馏、图像级多尺度 TTA 和强光度增强均有助于性能提升，其中光度增强贡献最大。

Abstract: We describe our 4th-place entry to the ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge, which reached a composite mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) of 69.73% on the official 1,815-image test set. Our model adapts the image encoder of a recent visual foundation model, Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3), with a lightweight decoder. Beyond this, we contribute two techniques and one empirical finding: (i) a self-distillation scheme that re-uses SAM3 itself, prompted with ground-truth boxes, as a teacher on the classes where it outperforms our own model; (ii) an image-level multi-scale test-time augmentation scheme that restores multi-scale inference for a fixed-input-size model by rescaling the image rather than the model input; and (iii) the finding that an aggressive photometric distortion from a winning 2025 GOOSE 2D entry, transplanted onto our pipeline, is its single largest source of improvement.

</details>


### [62] [TriFlow: Generating Artist-Like 3D Mesh Topology via Nearest-Vertex Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20131)
*Haoxuan Li,Ziya Erkoç,Daniele Sirigatti,Vladislav Rosov,Lei Li,Angela Dai,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriFlow 提出一种从输入几何条件直接生成紧凑三角网格的新方法，能产生更接近艺术家建模习惯的三角拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式网格生成/重建方法往往难以同时保证几何贴合、紧凑性与高质量拓扑连接，生成的三角网格拓扑不够结构化，泛化能力和效率也存在不足。

Method: 论文将网格拓扑表示为定义在表面上的最近顶点向量场（NVF），每个表面点在局部重心坐标框架中编码其与最近三角顶点的关联；随后训练潜在流匹配生成模型，在输入几何条件（如有符号距离场）约束下合成 NVF；最后通过基于 NVF 的表面区域聚类，并结合带约束的二次误差度量（QEM）网格简化与拓扑感知优化，提取一致的三角网格。

Result: 实验表明，TriFlow 相比当前先进的学习式方法具有更强泛化能力和显著更好的拓扑质量，同时 Chamfer Distance 降低约 90%，速度提升约 8 倍。

Conclusion: TriFlow 证明了用生成式 NVF 表示拓扑并结合拓扑感知网格优化，可以高效地产生几何精确、结构化且具有艺术家风格连接关系的紧凑 3D 三角网格。

Abstract: We present TriFlow, a new generative approach for producing compact 3D meshes with artist-like triangle topology directly from input geometry conditions such as signed distance fields. Our key insight is to represent mesh topology as a nearest-vertex vector field (NVF) defined over the surface, where each point encodes its association to the nearest triangle vertex in the local barycentric frame. We train a latent flow-matching model to synthesize this field, enabling topology generation conditioned on the input geometry. To extract a coherent mesh, we cluster surface regions using the generated NVF and guide a constrained quadric error metric (QEM) mesh simplification with topology-aware optimization. This yields output meshes that closely match the input geometry while exhibiting structured, artist-like connectivity. Experiments demonstrate that TriFlow achieves stronger generalization and significantly improved topology quality compared to state-of-the-art learning-based approaches, alongside 90% lower Chamfer Distance and an 8x speedup.

</details>


### [63] [SA-VIS: Sparse frame Annotations for training Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20140)
*Edoardo Mello Rella,Ajad Chhatkuli,Shipra Jain,Ender Konukoglu,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-VIS，通过Past-frames Feature Propagation（PFP）和轻量级帧特定实例查询，在稀疏帧标注条件下实现接近密集标注训练的视频实例分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线/半在线视频实例分割方法依赖长视频序列的密集逐帧标注进行训练，计算成本和标注成本都很高。作者希望证明：有效建模视频中实例及其时序演化并不一定需要密集标注帧。

Method: 提出Past-frames Feature Propagation（PFP）模块，从多帧图像编码器中聚合低维特征，以低计算开销实现跨帧信息传播；同时结合轻量级的帧特定Instance Queries，构建Sparse frame Annotation VIS（SA-VIS），支持使用稀疏视频帧标签进行端到端训练。

Result: SA-VIS在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021/2022和OVIS数据集上相较基线取得显著提升。在仅使用数据集中1/5图像标注时，性能相比密集标注训练仅下降0.4%；在有限标注场景下，相比现有最优方法AP提升超过1%。

Conclusion: SA-VIS以简单、低计算复杂度的设计有效缩小了稀疏标注与密集标注训练之间的性能差距，说明视频实例分割可以在显著降低标注与训练成本的同时保持较强性能。

Abstract: Recent online video instance segmentation (VIS) methods have achieved impressive results, thus becoming the preferred approach to segment instances in videos. Despite the resurgence of impressive single image models, the online (or semi-online) VIS approaches outperform single-image models (e.g., based on SAM) by using long sequences of densely annotated frames during training. However,such a training setup of VIS is expensive in the sense of compute as well as dense annotations required. In order to solve these major flaws, we argue that the effective modeling of the instances and their evolution in videos do not require densely annotated frames. To that end, we propose a simple and effective module, called Past-frames Feature Propagation (PFP) which aggregates low-dimensional features from the image encoder of multiple frames. This simple low-compute module provides tremendous learning capability in using sparse video frame labels for end-to-end training. Combined with a light-weight frame-specific Instance Queries, our Sparse frame Annotation VIS (SA-VIS) significantly improves performance over its baseline. Most interestingly, our simple design that avoids complexities effectively bridges the gap in accuracy between training on sparsely and densely annotated video sequences. This translates to a mere 0.4% drop in performance of SA-VIS when using annotations for only 1/5 of the images in the dataset. Empirically, SA-VIS shows strong improvements over the baseline on YouTube-VIS 2019/2021/2022 and Occluded VIS (OVIS) and an over 1% improvement in AP on the state-of-the-art in a limited annotations scenario.

</details>


### [64] [HEad and neCK TumOR (HECKTOR) 2025: Benchmark of Segmentation, Diagnosis, and Prognosis in Multimodal PET/CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20143)
*Numan Saeed,Salma Hassan,Shahad Hardan,Lishan Cai,Xinglong Liang,Moona Mazher,Abdul Qayyum,Yansong Bu,Mengye Lyu,Yue Lin,Mingyuan Meng,Chuanyi Huang,Lisheng Wang,Dalal Chamseddine,Shamimeh Ahrari,Beining Wu,Yifei Chen,Fuyou Mao,Hao Zhang,Baixiang Zhao,Surajit Ray,Muzi Guo,Lei Xiang,Jakob Dexl,Michael Ingrisch,Adrien Depeursinge,Arman Rahmim,Mathieu Hatt,Vincent Andrearczyk,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HECKTOR 2025挑战构建了基于多模态PET/CT影像与电子健康记录的头颈部肿瘤自动分析基准，涵盖肿瘤/淋巴结分割、复发无生存预测和HPV状态分类三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头颈部肿瘤放疗规划依赖精准靶区勾画，但口咽部解剖复杂、影像表现异质，人工分割耗时且观察者间差异大；同时，利用非侵入性影像预测长期临床结局和HPV状态具有重要临床价值但仍具挑战。

Method: 该研究组织HECKTOR 2025挑战赛，使用来自全球10个中心、超过1100例患者的多机构数据集，整合PET/CT影像和电子健康记录；参赛团队针对GTVp/GTVn分割、RFS预测和HPV分类提交算法，并在保留测试集上统一评估。

Result: 共有35支团队注册，15份最终提交被评估。最佳算法在分割任务上平均Dice系数为0.75，生存预测一致性指数为0.66，HPV分类平衡准确率为0.56。

Conclusion: 该挑战提供了一个较大规模、多机构、跨任务的自动化头颈部肿瘤分析基准；分割任务表现相对较好，而预后预测和HPV分类仍有明显提升空间，结果对自动化肿瘤工作流和临床决策支持的转化具有参考意义。

Abstract: Head and neck cancers (HNC) represent a significant global health burden, with accurate tumor delineation being essential for effective radiotherapy planning. The complexity of the oropharyngeal anatomy, combined with the heterogeneous appearance of tumors on imaging, makes manual segmentation time-intensive and subject to inter-observer variability. Beyond segmentation, predicting long-term clinical outcomes, such as recurrence-free survival (RFS), and determining human papillomavirus (HPV) status from noninvasive imaging, remain challenging yet clinically valuable goals. The HECKTOR 2025 challenge addresses these needs by establishing a comprehensive benchmark for automated HNC analysis using multimodal PET/CT imaging and electronic health records. Building on previous editions (2020-2022), this challenge features an expanded multi-institutional dataset comprising over 1,100 patients from 10 centers worldwide. Participants were tasked with three complementary objectives: (1) segmenting primary gross tumor volumes (GTVp) and metastatic lymph nodes (GTVn), (2) predicting recurrence-free survival, and (3) classifying HPV status. The challenge attracted 35 registered teams, with 15 final submissions evaluated on a held-out test set. Top-performing algorithms achieved a mean Dice similarity coefficient of 0.75 for segmentation, a concordance index of 0.66 for survival prediction, and a balanced accuracy of 0.56 for HPV classification. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of the submitted methodologies, evaluates their performance across different lesion characteristics, and discusses their implications for clinical translation in automated oncology workflows and decision support systems.

</details>


### [65] [NAMESAKES: Probing Identity Memorization in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20155)
*Morris Alper,Vasudha Varadarajan,Moran Yanuka,Angelina Wang,Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全黑盒的行为探针，用于在无需参考照片、训练数据或模型内部访问的情况下，判断文本到图像模型生成的人脸更可能是身份记忆还是虚构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖真实照片、训练集信息或白盒模型访问来判断生成脸是否属于训练记忆，实际应用受限；同时，T2I 模型可能在输入姓名时生成特定个人的逼真相貌，引发隐私风险。

Method: 作者构建 NAMESAKES 数据集，包含一千多个公众人物姓名与人脸，覆盖不同知名度，并加入扰动后的较不知名姓名；随后设计黑盒行为探针，仅通过模型输入输出行为来区分被记忆、未识别或虚构生成的身份。

Result: 在多个先进 T2I 模型上的实验表明，该探针能够显著预测身份记忆现象，并有效区分被记忆姓名与未被识别姓名，同时揭示不同模型家族之间的差异。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种更实用的隐私审计方法，可在缺乏训练数据和模型内部信息的条件下评估 T2I 模型中的身份记忆风险。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models generate realistic likenesses of some individuals when prompted with their names, raising privacy concerns. However, distinguishing whether a generated face is memorized or fabricated currently requires ground-truth photos, access to training data, or white-box access to model internals, limiting applicability. We introduce a fully black-box behavioral probe that distinguishes between these regimes while requiring no reference photos or prior knowledge of training data. To benchmark this task, we present the NAMESAKES dataset of over one thousand names and faces of public figures spanning a wide range of fame levels, along with perturbed, less famous names. Experiments on state-of-the-art T2I models show that our probe substantially predicts identity memorization and separates memorized from unrecognized names, with further insights into differences across model families.

</details>


### [66] [ARTEMIS: Agent-guided Reliability-aware Temporal Mask Evolution for Imperfectly Supervised Video Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20161)
*Tong Wang,Siwen Wang,Yaolei Qi,Jinxing Zhou,Yuting He,Guanyu Yang,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARTEMIS 是一个面向不完美监督视频息肉分割的统一框架，通过可靠性评估与时间维度的掩码演化提升弱监督/半监督条件下的分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频息肉分割在临床中需要稠密且时间一致的分割掩码，但像素级标注成本高。弱标注或少量稠密标注虽更经济，却面临低对比度、边界模糊、运动模糊、高光反射及监督稀疏等问题。直接使用 SAM2 生成伪标签容易出现边界泄漏、几何退化，并且未充分利用时间一致性与标签可靠性。

Method: ARTEMIS 首先利用 SAM2 将点/涂鸦等弱监督转换为粗掩码，同时将已有稠密标签作为可靠锚点；随后引入 debate-and-judge 视觉语言智能体，在弱监督场景下选择可靠的时间锚点，并通过 SAM2 进行双向传播以细化不可靠或未标注帧；最后采用时间可靠性感知的鲁棒学习训练分割器，包括可靠性引导的参考选择、参考原型传输模块和可靠性感知鲁棒损失，以评估掩码可靠性、跨帧传递目标身份并降低噪声监督的影响。

Result: 在 SUN-SEG 和 CVC-ClinicDB-612 数据集上，在涂鸦、点标注和有限稠密标签等设置下，ARTEMIS 均取得了当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在不完美监督视频息肉分割中，结合 SAM2、视觉语言智能体、时间锚点传播和可靠性感知鲁棒训练，可以有效缓解伪标签噪声与时间不一致问题，并显著提升分割表现。

Abstract: Imperfectly supervised video polyp segmentation (VPS) aims to learn dense, temporally consistent masks from inexpensive supervision, including weak annotations (points, scribbles) and semi-supervision with few densely labeled frames. This setting is clinically valuable but challenging due to weak contrast, ambiguous boundaries, motion blur, and specular highlights, compounded by sparse pixel-level guidance. While SAM2 can generate dense masks from sparse inputs, direct pseudo-labeling often yields geometry-degraded masks with boundary leakage, underutilizes temporal consistency, and ignores reliability. To address these issues, we propose ARTEMIS, a unified framework for imperfectly supervised VPS driven by agent-guided reliability-aware temporal mask evolution. ARTEMIS initializes coarse masks from available supervision: SAM2 converts points/scribbles, while dense labels serve as reliable anchors. A debate-and-judge vision-language agent selects reliable temporal anchors under weak supervision, which are propagated bidirectionally with SAM2 to refine unreliable or unlabeled frames. Finally, ARTEMIS trains the segmenter using temporal reliability-aware robust learning, incorporating reliability-guided reference selection, a Reference Prototype Transport Module, and reliability-aware robust loss. These components assess mask reliability, evolve anchors over time, transport target identity across frames, and down-weight noisy supervision instead of discarding difficult samples. Experiments on SUN-SEG and CVC-ClinicDB-612 under scribble, point, and limited-label settings demonstrate that ARTEMIS achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code will be released at https://github.com/wangtong627/ARTEMIS.

</details>


### [67] [Evaluating and Enhancing Negation Comprehension in Remote Sensing MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20177)
*Haochen Han,Jue Wang,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Fangming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向遥感多模态大语言模型否定理解的基准 RS-Neg，并提出测试时学习方法 NeFo，以提升模型对“非/不存在/错误”等否定语义的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大语言模型在多种任务中表现突出，但对否定表达的理解研究不足；真实应用中常需明确识别不存在或不满足条件的对象/区域，例如寻找未被淹没的撤离路线，因此需要系统评估并改进模型的否定理解能力。

Method: 作者构建 RS-Neg 基准，覆盖区域级到场景级任务；设计自动化数据生成流程，利用大语言模型合成多样化否定查询，并引入动态视觉聚焦模块进行验证。同时提出 NeFo，一种测试时学习方法，在模型优化中显式建模否定的逻辑作用，仅使用少量未标注测试样本进行适配。

Result: 实验表明，先进遥感多模态大语言模型在否定理解上存在明显困难，容易产生幻觉并出现显著性能下降；NeFo 使用约 5% 未标注测试样本即可显著提升模型否定理解能力，并能泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了遥感 MLLM 在否定理解方面的重要短板，并通过 RS-Neg 与 NeFo 分别提供了评估工具和改进方案，对提升模型在真实遥感场景中的可靠性具有意义。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in various Remote Sensing (RS) tasks. However, their ability to comprehend negation remains underexplored, limiting deployment in real-world applications where models must explicitly identify what is false or absent, e.g., emergency responders need to locate non-flooded routes for evacuation. To comprehensively study this limitation, we introduce RS-Neg, the first benchmark to evaluate negation understanding across region-level to scene-level tasks. Specifically, we design an automated data generation pipeline for RS imagery, using LLMs to synthesize diverse negation queries, and introduce a dynamic visual focus module for verification. Our evaluation reveals that advanced RS MLLMs struggle with negation, exhibiting hallucinations and substantial performance degradation. To close this gap, we propose NeFo, a novel test-time learning method that explicitly incorporates the logical role of negation into the model optimization. Remarkably, using about 5\% unlabeled test samples, NeFo significantly improves the negation understanding of models and shows strong generalization to unseen tasks. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [68] [HilDA: Hierarchical Distillation with Diffusion for Advancing Self-Supervised LiDAR Pre-trainin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20189)
*Maciej Wozniak,Jesper Ericsson,Hariprasath Govindarajan,Truls Nyberg,Thomas Gustafsson,Patric Jensfelt,Olov Andersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HilDA 是一种面向 LiDAR 骨干网络的自监督预训练框架，通过更充分利用视觉基础模型的层级语义、全局上下文以及 LiDAR 序列的时空信息，提升自动驾驶相关 3D 感知任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 camera-to-LiDAR 知识蒸馏方法通常将视觉基础模型视为黑盒教师，只进行逐帧特征相似性对齐，未充分利用教师模型的层级语义结构、场景级全局上下文，以及 LiDAR 序列中丰富的时空几何信息；同时自动驾驶场景中标注数据稀缺，难以覆盖真实世界复杂的几何和运动多样性。

Method: 论文提出 HilDA 自监督预训练框架，核心包括两部分：1）层级蒸馏：通过多层蒸馏实现逐步语义对齐，并通过全局上下文蒸馏学习场景级语义；2）时间占据扩散目标：利用 LiDAR 序列促进时空一致性，使模型同时捕获驾驶任务所需的语义信息（what）和几何位置信息（where）。

Result: 使用 HilDA 预训练的模型在跨模态蒸馏基准上达到当前最佳表现，并且在 3D 目标检测、场景流估计和语义占据预测等任务上优于既有蒸馏方法训练的模型。

Conclusion: HilDA 表明，将视觉基础模型的层级语义与全局上下文信息、LiDAR 序列的时空一致性联合用于自监督预训练，可以显著增强 LiDAR 表征学习能力，并提升多种自动驾驶 3D 感知任务的泛化与下游性能。

Abstract: Leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) for camera-to-LiDAR knowledge distillation offers a promising solution to the scarcity of annotated data needed to represent the immense geometric and kinematic diversity of real-world autonomous driving (AD). However, current approaches typically treat VFMs as black-box teachers, relying exclusively on frame-wise feature similarity. Consequently, they do not fully exploit the teacher's layer-wise semantic structure and global context, as well as the rich spatiotemporal information inherent in LiDAR sequences. We propose HilDA, a self-supervised pretraining framework for LiDAR backbones that better captures the semantic what and geometric where needed for driving tasks. HilDA combines hierarchical distillation comprising multi-layer distillation for progressive semantic alignment and global context distillation for scene-level semantics, with a temporal occupancy diffusion objective promoting spatiotemporal consistency. Models pre-trained with HilDA achieve state-of-the-art results on cross-modal distillation benchmarks and outperform models trained via prior distillation approaches on 3D object detection, scene flow, and semantic occupancy prediction. Code available at: https://maxiuw.github.io/hilda.

</details>


### [69] [Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long: Exploring Dataset Distillation for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20196)
*Hyun-Kurl Jang,Jihun Kim,Hyeokjun Kweon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DO-ALL 通过数据集蒸馏生成少量合成源域锚点，在不保留原始源数据的情况下，为持续测试时自适应提供稳定源信息，从而提升长期分布漂移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTTA 需要在无标签、持续变化的目标域中在线维持模型性能；但实际部署中常因隐私或许可限制无法保存源数据，而完全无源的 CTTA 容易在长期漂移中出现自训练误差累积和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 论文提出 DO-ALL 框架：部署前使用数据集蒸馏生成紧凑的合成蒸馏锚点以概括源分布；部署时为每个目标样本匹配语义最相关的锚点，并通过源回放、表示对齐和流形平滑正则化为不同 CTTA 方法提供稳定参考。

Result: DO-ALL 可作为即插即用模块集成到现有 CTTA 算法中，并在 CIFAR100-C、ImageNet-C 和 CCC benchmark 上持续提升长期鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用数据集蒸馏保存紧凑且隐私友好的源域信息，是在不保留原始源数据前提下实现稳定、长期测试时自适应的有效方向。

Abstract: Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) aims to maintain model performance under evolving target domains by adapting online without labeled data. However, practical deployments often cannot retain the source dataset due to privacy or licensing constraints, and purely source-free CTTA methods tend to become unstable under long-term distribution shift, suffering from compounding self-training errors and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce DO-ALL (Distill Once, Adapt Life-Long), a plug-and-play framework that revisits source information in a compact and privacy-conscious form via Dataset Distillation (DD). Before deployment, DO-ALL performs DD to produce a small set of synthetic distilled anchors that summarize the source distribution. During adaptation, each target sample is matched with its most semantically aligned anchor, which provides a stable reference for various CTTA via source replay, representation alignment, and manifold-smoothing regularization. DO-ALL can be seamlessly integrated into existing CTTA algorithms, consistently improving long-term robustness across CIFAR100-C, ImageNet-C, and the CCC benchmark. This demonstrates the potential of leveraging DD to enable stable and continuous adaptation without retaining raw source data. The code is available at https://github.com/blue-531/DOALL.

</details>


### [70] [Evaluation of Image Matching for Art Skills Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20199)
*Asaad Alghamdi,Michael Poor,Trung-Nghia Le,Tam V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将手绘图与原始模板进行图像相似度匹配来评估绘画技能，并比较了 SIFT 特征与 Siamese 网络两种方法；结果显示该任务可行，且 SIFT 关键点匹配更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统绘画技能评估需要人工综合判断，过程繁琐、主观且耗时。随着计算机视觉的发展，研究者希望用自动化图像相似度分析来模拟人工评估，降低评估成本并提高效率。

Method: 以原始模板作为参考图像，将被评估者的手绘图与模板进行匹配；分别实现并分析 SIFT 特征匹配方法和 Siamese 网络方法，用于度量两幅图像之间的相似性，从而推断绘画技能水平。

Result: 实验结果表明，利用计算机视觉方法评估绘画技能是可行的；在特征分析中，SIFT 基于关键点的匹配比 Siamese 网络表现出更有效的绘画技能检测能力。

Conclusion: 论文认为，基于模板-手绘图相似度的自动评估可以用于绘画技能测量，其中 SIFT 关键点匹配是较为有效的方案，但摘要未充分说明数据集规模、评价指标和泛化能力。

Abstract: While some individuals possess a natural talent for drawing, mastering this skill requires dedicated training and practice. Determining one's skill in the art of drawing requires proper comprehensive assessment. In this paper, we propose a method to measure drawing skill by by matching the hand-drawn image with the original template. Existing techniques often involve complex processes. However, advancements in computer vision allow us to train computers to perform these comparisons at a human-like level, thereby resolving the tedious and overwhelming traditional process. Using computer vision applications, determining image similarity involves identifying the level of similarities in an image with a reference image. We have implemented and analyzed the SIFT feature and Siamese network to measure image similarity. Our results indicate that it is feasible to assess art skill levels. Through feature analysis, we found that SIFT-based key point matching provides a more effective means of detecting drawing skills.

</details>


### [71] [Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20477)
*Yusuf Salcan,Simon Ging,Robin Schirrmeister,Philipp Arnold,Elmar Kotter,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Thomas Brox*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RefRad2D 数据集与 RadGrounder 模型，用于在缺少人工空间标注的情况下训练放射学视觉语言模型，实现报告生成、VQA 与空间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学 VLM 需要具备视觉可验证的空间定位能力，但人工空间标注成本高、规模化困难；同时医学 VQA 与报告生成模型往往缺乏可靠的病灶/结构定位监督。

Method: 构建大规模双语德英 RefRad2D 数据集，包含 120 万 CT/MR 图像-文本对，并通过 LLM 筛选整理与自动分割生成任务特定的 VQA 和空间 grounding 子集；在该数据上训练 RadGrounder，使其联合完成报告生成、视觉问答以及基于边界框或分割的空间定位。

Result: RadGrounder 在 Slake、VQA-RAD 等外部 VQA 基准上达到与专用医学 VLM 竞争的性能；将临床数据加入训练混合后，相比仅在下游数据集微调，开放式 VQA 表现进一步提升；加入 grounding 监督未损害语言质量或 VQA 性能。

Conclusion: RefRad2D 可迁移地提升医学 VLM 的开放式问答能力，RadGrounder 证明了无需人工空间标注也能训练具备空间可验证输出的放射学 VLM，且不会牺牲语言任务表现。

Abstract: We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.

</details>


### [72] [DeepForestVisionV2: Ecology-Driven Taxonomy Expansion for Camera-Trap Monitoring in African Tropical Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20223)
*Hugo Magaldi,Theau d'Audiffret,Etienne Francois Akomo-Okoue,Bala Amarasekaran,Naomi Anderson,Claire Auger,Noemie Cappelle,Daniel Cornelis,Raphael Cornette,Tobias Deschner,Gabriel Dubus,Davy Fonteyn,Rosa M. Garriga,Jennifer Hatlauf,Innocent Kasekendi,Raymond Katumba,Aram Kazandjian,Alfred Ngomanda,Stephan Ntie,Simone Pika,Xavier Rufray,Harold Rugonge,John Justice Tibesigwa,Peter van Lunteren,Hadrien Vanthomme,Joeri A.
