The promise is that they either learn from interacting with the game, or from player data, to develop true human-like behavior. In this paper, we envision more applications of reinforcement learning for game AI in the future. For this to materialize, current research limitations are prohibitive to broad deployment across game genres. Therefore, we propose a framework for training reinforcement learning models with a set of requirements in mind that are suited towards game AI and game development. We present examples of games with reinforcement learning-augmented game AI and describe the practicalities of deploying player-facing machine learning agents in modern games. Furthermore, we identify bottlenecks and hard problems in these areas, which we believe offer promising research directions to accelerate the adoption of machine learning in game AI for the video game industry.

</details>


### [213] [QMFOL: Benchmarking Large Language Model Reasoning via Quantifiable Monadic First-Order Logic Test Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20227)
*Xinyi Zheng,Ling Shi,Tianlong Yu,Yongxin Zhao,Lorenz Goette,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 QMFOL：一种自动生成一元一阶逻辑推理任务的框架，并构建 QMFOLBench，用于以可控复杂度评估大语言模型的演绎推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理评测基准难以细粒度控制逻辑复杂度，也难以同时保证语义多样性与逻辑一致性；随着 LLM/LRM 推理能力提升，需要更可控、更可靠的演绎推理评估方法。

Method: 框架先通过合取与析取模式构造形式化的一元一阶逻辑结构，控制推理深度、宽度、标签类型和干扰项；再利用 LLM 将形式逻辑转写为自然语言，并通过外部定理证明器进行往返验证以确保逻辑一致性。基于此构建含 2880 个样本、960 种配置的 QMFOLBench。

Result: 在六个大型推理模型和两个大语言模型上的实验表明，随着逻辑复杂度上升，模型性能下降且计算开销增加；模型在 True 标签任务上表现优于 False 或 Unknown 标签任务，并且对语义变化较敏感。

Conclusion: QMFOL 提供了一种可扩展、可靠且复杂度可控的演绎推理基准构造方法，有助于更精细地评估现代语言模型的逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in reasoning, particularly in deductive reasoning, which is crucial for high-stakes decision-making. As models improve, evaluation benchmarks should evolve to keep pace. However, existing benchmarks lack fine-grained control over logical complexity and struggle to balance semantic diversity with logical consistency.
  To address these issues, we propose QMFOL, an automated framework for generating monadic first-order logic reasoning tasks with quantifiable and controllable complexity. It constructs formal logical structures using conjunction and disjunction patterns, enabling precise control over reasoning depth, width, label types, and distractors. These structures are then translated into natural language via LLMs, with logical consistency ensured through round-trip verification using an external prover. Based on our framework, we build QMFOLBench, a benchmark comprising 2880 instances with 960 configurations across diverse logical and semantic dimensions. Evaluations on six large reasoning models (LRMs) and two LLMs show that performance degrades and computational overhead increases with rising logical complexity. Models perform better on True-labeled tasks than on False or Unknown ones, and exhibit sensitivity to semantic variation. Overall, QMFOL offers a scalable and reliable approach for constructing deductive reasoning benchmarks with controllable complexity, enabling more precise evaluation of reasoning capabilities in modern language models.

</details>


### [214] [A Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20236)
*Federica Filippini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAMO，将约束优化中强化学习奖励权重的选择本身建模为学习问题，以减少人工调参并提升动态环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算与网络系统中的许多决策问题可表示为带性能约束的成本最小化问题。现有强化学习方法通常通过加权惩罚项将成本与约束违违反合并为单一标量奖励，但策略表现高度依赖人工设定的权重；在非平稳环境中，目标优化与约束满足之间的重要性会变化，使合适权衡更难确定。

Method: 提出 MAMO（Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization），采用多智能体强化学习，将任务执行与目标/奖励设计解耦；把奖励权重选择视为一个可学习问题，由智能体自适应调整不同目标或约束惩罚的权重。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验指标或定量结果，但表明该方法旨在更自动地寻找成本优化与约束违违反规避之间的平衡，适用于动态环境中的受约束优化。

Conclusion: MAMO 为动态环境下受约束优化的 RL 方案提供了更自主、稳健的方向，减少对手工奖励权重设计的依赖，是面向多目标约束优化的一步探索。

Abstract: Many decision-making problems in computing and networking systems can be naturally formulated as cost-minimization problems under performance constraints. In dynamic environments, reinforcement learning (RL) is often used to solve such problems at runtime by embedding both costs and constraint violations into a single scalar reward through weighted penalty terms, following a Lagrangian-inspired formulation. However, in this context the behavior of the learned policy critically depends on the choice of these weights, which are typically selected manually. This makes it difficult to identify an appropriate trade-off between optimizing the primary objective and effectively avoiding constraint violations, particularly in non-stationary environments where their relative importance may change. This paper presents MAMO (Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization), an approach to tackle this balancing problem through multi-agent RL. MAMO decouples task execution from objective design by formulating the selection of reward weights as a learning problem, providing a !rst step towards more autonomous and robust RL-based solutions for constrained optimization problems in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [215] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MACR 框架，用于解决大语言模型在内部参数知识与外部上下文之间、以及多个外部上下文之间的知识冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设模型知识或外部上下文之一可靠，并通过偏向某一来源来规避冲突，忽视了两者都可能存在错误以及多源外部信息之间也可能相互矛盾的问题。因此需要一种能够显式识别、分析并解决不一致性的机制。

Method: MACR 首先通过改进的语义熵估计模型对查询答案的置信度，并据此决定将模型内部知识外化为文本，或在内部知识不足时检索相关外部知识，形成基础上下文。随后引入归纳式多智能体推理框架，由三个专门智能体分别负责归纳显式规则、分析潜在冲突、解决所有上下文中的不一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，MACR 在多个基准测试上显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，并能为显式知识冲突提供可解释的解决过程。

Conclusion: MACR 超越了传统在模型知识与外部知识之间二选一的范式，通过自适应知识评估、检索与多智能体冲突推理机制，更有效且可解释地解决 LLM 知识冲突。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [216] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究用视觉模型自动评分学生生成的科学绘图，并通过置信度感知机制实现高置信样本自动评分、低置信样本人工复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学教育中，学生绘图可用于评估其概念理解，但人工评分依赖专家判断，成本高、难以在课堂和大规模评估中持续应用。因此需要一种可靠、可扩展且能控制风险的自动评分方法。

Method: 作者评估了采用参数高效适配的视觉 Transformer（ViT）模型，用于对学生科学绘图进行自动评分；同时提出置信度感知评分框架，从测试时预测分布中推导响应级置信度，并据此进行选择性自动化评分。

Result: 在六个符合 NGSS 的初中科学评估题目上，方法提升了评分可靠性，并能够在自动评分覆盖率与评分风险之间实现实用权衡。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的视觉模型可提高学生科学绘图自动评分的可信度和可部署性，适合用于辅助教育评估中的大规模、低成本评分流程。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [217] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lagrange：一种基于 Masked Latent Fields 的开放词汇、稀疏端到端自动驾驶框架，将 VLM 语义表示与连续能量场和拉格朗日动作最小化结合，以提升开放世界场景下的鲁棒性、可解释性和运动学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂开放世界自动驾驶需要同时具备异常场景泛化能力与满足车辆运动学约束的规划能力。现有稠密占用类方法几何鲁棒但计算开销大、语义推理弱；稀疏查询式规划高效但依赖闭集类别，面对 OOD 事件脆弱；VLA 虽具开放词汇推理能力，但自回归离散 token 生成与车辆连续高频控制需求不匹配。

Method: 提出 Lagrange 框架，利用 VLM 将类别无关的目标 proposal 编码为连续语义视觉 token；设计意图驱动的 masked cross-attention，对时间维度上的无关实体进行过滤；将关注后的 token 解码为空间坐标上的隐式连续能量场；再将决策建模为该能量场上的拉格朗日动作最小化问题，从而兼顾避障与车辆运动学约束。

Result: 在 nuScenes 标准基准和 CODA 长尾基准上的离线评估表明，该方法在开放世界自动驾驶任务中具有较好的鲁棒性、可解释性和运动学可行性，展示出优于传统闭集稀疏规划或高成本稠密建模范式的潜力。

Conclusion: Lagrange 通过开放词汇语义表示、稀疏计算结构、连续能量场建模和运动学约束优化，尝试统一感知泛化与可行轨迹规划，为开放世界端到端自动驾驶提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [218] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向强数据稀缺场景的振动故障诊断方法，通过周期性多激励级别过程将系统非线性响应转化为图像，并利用预训练CNN进行迁移学习诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习可提升智能故障诊断系统构建效率，但现有方法仍依赖大量标注数据；在机械或结构故障场景中，获取充足标注样本通常困难，因此需要适用于小样本/强数据稀缺条件的新型诊断方案。

Method: 作者设计了基于振动信号的IFDS框架：利用真实系统内在非线性，采用周期性多激励水平过程生成可视化图像；提出新的数据可视化方法及配套数据增强技术；再将生成图像输入预训练卷积神经网络，通过深度迁移学习完成故障诊断。

Result: 在铁路受电弓结构上的实验验证表明，该方法在数据稀缺条件下能够有效支持故障诊断，验证了所提可视化与增强策略结合预训练CNN的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将多激励振动响应、图像化表示、数据增强和预训练CNN迁移学习结合，可缓解智能故障诊断中标注数据不足的问题，适合强数据稀缺的结构健康监测与故障诊断场景。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [219] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SoftSkill：将自然语言技能文件压缩为短长度可训练软前缀，在冻结大模型上作为潜在行为先验使用；在多个任务上相比无技能提示和 SkillOpt 取得更好效果，但对长程 agentic 程序行为的压缩仍不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 技能通常以 Markdown 自然语言文件形式部署，虽然可读、可移植，但推理时需要冻结语言模型反复把冗长文本解释为行为策略，带来上下文开销和执行不稳定性。论文希望探索能否用紧凑的连续上下文对象替代长文本技能，使技能直接作为模型行为控制信号。

Method: 作者提出 SoftSkill：保持基础模型冻结，将自然语言技能初始化为一个紧凑连续上下文对象，并通过可训练的 soft delta 进行优化；训练目标为 next-token prediction。推理时使用长度较短的 SoftSkill 虚拟前缀作为 latent behavioral prior，而非输入大量 Markdown 技能 token。

Result: 在主要单轮设置中，长度为 32 的 SoftSkill prefix 相比无技能提示在 SearchQA、LiveMath、DocVQA 上分别提升 8.3、42.1、1.3 个百分点；相比 SkillOpt，在 SearchQA 和 LiveMath 上分别提升 5.2、12.5 个百分点，同时将数百到数千个 Markdown 技能 token 压缩为少量虚拟 token。对于更困难的 agentic execution，稀疏轨迹模仿能提供一定信号，但尚不能稳定压缩长程程序化行为。

Conclusion: 论文表明，部分任务技能不一定适合继续作为推理时需要重新解释的 Markdown 文本，而可以被建模为控制冻结模型进入任务状态的紧凑潜在变量；但该方法在复杂长程 agent 行为上的可靠性仍有待提升。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [220] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究能否从GUI交互轨迹中自动挖掘可解释的技能库，并用其提升下游计算机使用代理策略；结果显示可读性较好但迁移收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式技能库有助于提高计算机使用代理的可检查性，但尚不清楚这些技能能否从交互数据中自动挖掘，并进一步改善下游策略表现。

Method: 提出三阶段流程：先对GUI轨迹进行分段，再将片段聚类为候选技能，最后利用所得技能标注训练技能感知策略；并在源基准与跨域任务上评估聚类纯度和策略效果。

Result: 挖掘出的簇在源基准上具有一定可读性：8个簇中有5个相对于InteraSkill Workflows标签的纯度至少为0.95。但GRPO仅将IW技能步骤准确率从18.5%提升到20.5%，BrowseComp+几乎无变化，并且在关键源域指标上弱于简单频率先验。

Conclusion: 轨迹挖掘能够揭示可检查的技能结构，但当前边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以带来可靠的跨域策略提升；该工作更适合作为诊断性研究。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [221] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前基于 E2M1 的 FP4 训练存在由非均匀量化网格导致的 Shrinkage Bias，并提出统一网格的 UFP4 训练方案，在多种大模型预训练设置中优于 E2M1 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4 训练有望显著降低大语言模型预训练的显存与计算成本，但现有硬件与训练方案主要依赖 E2M1 格式。作者希望解释 E2M1 FP4 训练不稳定的根因，并探索更适合训练的 4-bit 数值格式。

Method: 作者从量化网格几何出发分析非均匀 E2M1 的系统性负舍入误差 Shrinkage Bias，研究其在层间累积以及与随机 Hadamard 变换（RHT）的相互作用；随后提出 UFP4，采用 E1M2/INT4 风格的均匀 4-bit 网格，对三个训练 GEMM 都使用 RHT，并仅对 dY 使用随机舍入。

Result: 在 Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B 和 MoE 124B 的长程预训练实验中，UFP4 相比强 E2M1 基线获得更低的相对 BF16 损失退化；缩放律分析与消融实验进一步支持了该设计。

Conclusion: 非均匀 E2M1 FP4 格式的网格几何会引入训练不稳定因素，而均匀 4-bit 网格能更好利用 RHT 提升量化质量。未来加速器应将 E1M2/INT4 式均匀 FP4/INT4 训练原语作为与 E2M1 并列的一等硬件支持。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [222] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合 EfficientNet-B0 与 CBAM 注意力模块的可解释深度学习框架，用于精子形态分类，并在两个公开数据集上取得优于基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育常与精子形态异常相关；现有深度学习方法虽可自动分析精子形态，但多数缺乏可解释性，限制了其在临床场景中的采用。

Method: 使用预训练 EfficientNet-B0 作为骨干网络，并加入卷积块注意力模块 CBAM，使模型关注精子头部等关键区域；同时采用 Grad-CAM++ 对模型决策依据进行可视化解释。

Result: 在 SMIDS 和 HuSHem 数据集上分别达到 90.2% 和 93.9% 的准确率，macro F1 分别为 0.913 和 0.948，性能优于 SimpleCNN 和标准 EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具较高分类性能与可解释性，可作为生育诊所中自动化精子形态分析的实用辅助工具。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [223] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用结构化的实验室环境时序特征提升IVF妊娠率预测，并显示这些环境信号具有跨诊所迁移价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往IVF妊娠率建模主要依赖患者层面变量，高分辨率实验室环境数据虽被持续采集但利用不足；直接使用传感器均值也难以捕捉培养箱微环境的动态波动，因此需要更有临床意义的环境特征表示。

Method: 作者从培养箱环境数据中构造55个上下文感知的时间特征，包括滚动热稳定性、温湿度同步达标情况、峰值压力持续时间以及压力后恢复速度等；随后在亚洲诊所数据上验证特征预测能力，并训练层级贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化在亚洲与北欧诊所间共享环境效应，同时保留站点特异性基线。

Result: 在亚洲IVF诊所61周数据上，工程化环境特征将交叉验证预测误差降至1.27%，优于原始传感器均值的3–5%。在北欧诊所留出数据上，模型达到R2=0.86，并在35–39岁年龄组相较朴素基线减少64%误差。

Conclusion: 结构化的培养箱环境监测数据包含具有临床意义且可迁移的预测信号；相较简单均值，动态时序特征和层级贝叶斯建模更适合刻画不同诊所间可共享与特异的环境影响。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [224] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文示例如何影响语言模型的有害服从行为，发现示例内容、顺序与训练方法共同决定模型是否更容易被示例诱导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，上下文演示可用于越狱语言模型，但尚不清楚模型如何理解不同类型的服从示例，尤其是良性服从示例与有害服从示例是否会产生相同影响。

Method: 作者混合良性服从演示（无害请求—有帮助回答）与有害服从演示（有害请求—有帮助回答），在四个模型上检验三种关于演示组成如何驱动有害服从的假设，并分析偏好优化、示例顺序以及拒答行为与上下文学习之间的关系。

Result: 实验发现良性与有害演示不可互换：良性演示可能降低也可能提高有害服从，具体取决于模型。偏好优化是阻止良性演示提升有害服从的关键训练阶段；示例顺序存在显著近因偏差；不同模型在拒答时对上下文信号的处理方式不同。

Conclusion: 该工作从“演示式越狱是否有效”推进到解释其机制：模型从服从演示中提取的信息取决于演示内容、排列顺序和训练流程，尤其是偏好优化对安全行为具有关键作用。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multi-LCB，将 LiveCodeBench 扩展到 12 种编程语言，用于更严格地评估大语言模型的跨语言代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiveCodeBench 虽能通过持续更新题目和按发布日期过滤来降低数据污染风险，并较全面地评估代码能力，但其评测主要局限于 Python，无法回答模型是否具备真实软件工程中所需的多编程语言泛化能力。

Method: 作者将 LCB 中的 Python 编程任务转换为其他编程语言中的等价任务，同时保留原有的污染控制机制和评测协议。Multi-LCB 与原 LCB 格式完全兼容，因此可自动跟踪未来 LCB 的更新。研究还在该基准上评估了 24 个指令型与推理型大语言模型。

Result: 实验发现当前模型存在 Python 过拟合、特定语言层面的潜在污染，以及多语言代码生成性能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB 弥补了 LCB 仅限 Python 的核心不足，为多编程语言代码生成评估提供了更严格的基准，并揭示了当前大语言模型在跨语言代码能力上的关键短板。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [226] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit 是一种用于冻结的 flow-matching 文本转语音系统的终身自适应框架，通过学习文本嵌入空间中的潜在条件编辑来修正专有名词发音，而无需更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 flow-matching TTS 具有较强零样本合成质量，但部署后是静态的；对于未登录专有名词的发音错误，通常只能通过重新训练模型解决，成本高且不适合持续纠错。

Method: 在收到纠错反馈后，FlowEdit 优化 token 级文本嵌入扰动，将发音修正表示为潜在条件编辑；随后把该修正存入作为内容可寻址情景记忆的 Modern Hopfield Network。推理时通过软注意力和相似度门控检索相关修正，从而支持模糊的形态匹配。

Result: 在包含 312 个多语专有名词、覆盖 18 个语系的自建基准上，FlowEdit 相比零样本基线将目标词音素错误率相对降低 92.7%，同时保持通用语音质量不变；单张 GPU 上每次纠错约 15 秒完成。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在不更新 TTS 主模型参数的情况下，可通过可检索的潜在编辑记忆实现快速、持续、可泛化的发音纠错，适合部署后个性化与长期维护场景。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [227] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeepSWIP：面向 DeepProbLog 的单世界反事实语义，通过神经物化、SWIP 转换与加权模型计数，在单个变换程序中精确计算反事实查询，并在实验中验证其正确性与效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号系统（如 DeepProbLog）能结合神经感知与概率逻辑，但标准推理主要是关联性的；反事实推理需要明确的因果语义，以处理干预、证据以及神经谓词带来的不确定性。

Method: 作者将固定上下文中的神经谓词通过神经物化转化为普通 ProbLog 随机选择，随后应用 Single World Intervention Programs（SWIPs）构造单世界干预程序，并将反事实推理归约为单个变换程序上的加权模型计数（WMC）。同时利用 ProbLog 条件概率的 quotient-WMC 形式分析活跃神经概率、干预清理、校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定性。

Result: 在有限 grounding 和唯一支持模型假设下，DeepSWIP 相对于学习到的物化函数因果模型是精确的。MPI3D 实验中，方法在 12,000 个查询上与 DeepTwin 构造结果一致，并因避免 Twin 模型的内生变量复制获得 2.14 倍推理加速。SUMO HOV 实验显示神经校准退化会使 plug-in 估计产生偏差，而正确限定作用域的随机策略 AIPW 估计器能去除人口均值和 ATE 估计中的大部分一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP 为 DeepProbLog 中的反事实推理提供了形式化、精确且更高效的单世界因果语义框架，并揭示了神经校准与证据稀有性对反事实估计稳定性的影响。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [228] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LedgerAgent，在推理时显式维护任务状态账本，并在执行会改变环境的工具调用前进行状态相关策略检查，从而提升客服场景工具调用智能体的合规性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客服领域的工具调用智能体需要跨多轮对话维护事实、标识符、约束和条件，并遵守领域策略。传统方法把观察、工具返回和策略说明都放入提示词中，让模型每次自行重建状态，容易导致使用过期、缺失或错误信息，或生成虽语法正确但违反当前状态相关策略的工具调用。

Method: 提出 LedgerAgent：在推理阶段将已观察到的任务状态单独维护在一个 ledger 中，并把该状态渲染回提示词供模型决策使用；同时在执行会改变环境的工具调用前，利用 ledger 检查状态依赖的策略约束，若违反策略则阻止执行。

Result: 在四个客服领域以及包含开源和闭源模型的混合评测面板上，相比标准的基于提示词的工具调用方法，LedgerAgent 提升了平均 pass@k，且在更严格的多次试验一致性指标下收益最大。

Conclusion: 显式状态管理与执行前策略校验能够缓解工具调用智能体在多轮客服任务中的状态漂移和策略违规问题，提高合规性、可靠性和多次运行一致性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [229] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用跨注意力归因方法，分析风格描述文本中不同词语如何影响语音扩散式TTS的声学输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本到语音系统可用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词语如何影响生成语音仍不清楚；理解这一机制有助于诊断系统失效模式并提升表达式TTS的可控性。

Method: 作者将DAAM框架首次适配到语音扩散模型，提出cross-attention attribution方法，并在CapSpeech-TTS上提取25层、24个ODE步中的逐token热力图；实验覆盖120条风格描述与30条文本转录组合，共3600组生成条件。

Result: 实验发现：风格词的时间方差低于内容词/功能词，体现全局条件作用；风格注意力与F0和能量相关；风格条件作用在早期ODE步和深层网络中最强；第17层注意力熵最低，同时出现风格重要性峰值，表明该层具有最高网络选择性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统揭示自然语言风格描述如何通过跨注意力影响语音扩散模型，为解释、诊断和改进可控表达式TTS提供了分析工具和实证依据。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [230] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究分布偏移下混合专家模型（MoE）中专家校准与路由机制的关系，指出硬路由中专家校准可保证整体校准，而软路由中并不足够，并提出对抗性重加权方法改善准确率—校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于理解和信任模型输出概率至关重要。已有研究表明，对单个预测器实施校准可提升集成模型，尤其是MoE的准确性和校准性，但在什么条件下校准能帮助MoE仍缺乏系统理解，尤其是在分布偏移场景中。

Method: 作者从理论上分析MoE在分布偏移下的行为，重点考察路由机制与专家级校准之间的相互作用；区分硬路由与软路由情形，并进一步提出一种对抗性重加权方法，用于惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合预测的校准误差。

Result: 研究发现，在硬路由MoE中，专家校准足以在一大类分布偏移下保证整体模型校准；但在软路由MoE中，专家级校准不足以保证整体校准。所提出的对抗性重加权方法在不同模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移设置下，均改善了平均表现及困难数据子集上的准确率—校准权衡。

Conclusion: 论文表明，MoE的校准效果取决于路由机制与分布偏移的交互；仅校准专家并不总是充分，尤其对软路由模型而言。通过面向分布偏移下聚合输出的对抗性校准优化，可以更稳健地提升MoE模型的可靠性和预测性能。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Ricci flow for the Bures--Helstrom qubit metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19493)
*Andrew Lesniewski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文显式刻画了量子比特态空间上 Bures–Helstrom 度量的 Ricci 流：该度量因与三维单位圆球的测地半球等距且为 Einstein 度量，几何 Ricci 流表现为同伦收缩，并在时间 T=1/4 塌缩；体积归一化流下它是固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bures–Helstrom 度量是量子比特态空间上的最小单调 Riemann 度量，理解其 Ricci 流有助于揭示量子信息几何中单调度量锥、曲率演化和规范选择之间的关系。

Method: 作者利用量子 Fisher 归一化将 Bloch 球识别为三维单位圆球的测地半球；在一般旋转对称规范下建立径向 lapse 与 warping factor 的耦合流方程，并通过 Hamilton–DeTurck 规范将问题化为移动 DeTurck 框架中的线性强迫热方程 D_tΨ=Ψ_{ss}-2；同时分析固定 lapse 坐标中的输运项。

Result: 由于 Bures–Helstrom 度量是 Einstein 度量，Ricci 流为 g(t)=(1-4t)g_BH，标量曲率为 6/(1-4t)，消亡时间 T=1/4；在 t<T 内度量保持在单调锥中，只在极限塌缩时离开非退化 Riemann 度量锥。体积归一化流下该度量为固定点，线性化算子为 Δ_{S^3}+3，谱为 σ_l=-(l-1)(l+3)，去除尺度模后谱隙为 5。

Conclusion: 论文表明 Bures–Helstrom 度量的 Ricci 流结构高度刚性：未归一化流仅发生同伦收缩，归一化流中则保持静止；Hamilton–DeTurck 规范提供了将旋转对称流简化为线性方程的有效方式，并给出稳定性谱信息。

Abstract: The Bures--Helstrom metric is the minimal monotone Riemannian metric on the state space of a qubit. With the quantum Fisher normalization used here, it identifies the Bloch ball with a geodesic hemisphere of the unit round three--sphere. We describe its Ricci flow explicitly. In a general rotationally symmetric gauge the flow is a coupled system for the radial lapse and warping factor; a single scalar equation appears only after a Hamilton--DeTurck gauge choice. In the corresponding moving DeTurck frame the squared warping function $Ψ=Φ^2$ satisfies the linear forced heat equation \begin{equation*} D_tΨ=Ψ_{ss}-2, \end{equation*} while the fixed-lapse coordinate form contains the associated transport term. Since the Bures--Helstrom metric is Einstein, the geometric flow itself is the homothetic shrinker \begin{equation*} g(t)=(1-4t)g_{\mathrm{BH}}, \end{equation*} with scalar curvature $6/(1-4t)$ and extinction time $T=1/4$. Thus the metric remains inside the monotone cone for all $t<T$ and leaves the cone of nondegenerate Riemannian metrics only through the collapsed limit. We also record the volume--normalized flow, for which the Bures--Helstrom metric is a fixed point. Its linearization is the shifted round--sphere Laplacian $Δ_{\mathbb S^3}+3$, with spectrum \begin{equation*} σ_\ell=-(\ell-1)(\ell+3), \end{equation*} and spectral gap $5$ after removal of the scaling mode.

</details>


### [232] [Lightweight Non-Line-of-Sight Channel Detection for ML-assisted Bluetooth Direction Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19497)
*Hamed Talebian,Aamir Mahmood,Mehdi Haghshenas,Stefani Rydbloom,Peter Karlsson,Mikael Gidlund*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对BLE测向在多径环境下LOS/NLOS识别困难的问题，提出基于CTE IQ特征的轻量级机器学习流程，利用稳健标准化、PCA/AKDE分析、Nyström核近似与SVC实现高效分类，并在自建标注数据集上验证其精度与复杂度折中优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE室内工业定位在多径反射和散射环境中角度估计易受偏置影响；相比宽带无线电，BLE测向缺乏适合窄带信道的特征表示、可扩展核特征变换方法，以及面向轻量化数据驱动LOS/NLOS分类的专用数据集。

Method: 构建受控BLE测量平台，在两类传播环境中采集带标签LOS/NLOS数据；对BLE CTE IQ特征进行分位数稳健标准化；使用PCA和自适应核密度估计分析场景相关统计特性与可分性；采用Nyström Kernel Approximation生成低秩非线性特征映射，并接入轻量SVC分类头，同时与随机森林和多层感知机进行对比；最后利用校准后的后验概率进行代价敏感阈值选择。

Result: Nyström核近似相较原始基线带来约7%–14%的准确率提升；MLP取得更高的绝对准确率，但Nyström–SVC在训练复杂度、推理开销和内存占用之间表现出更优折中。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了面向BLE CTE IQ窄带特征的稳健预处理与低秩核映射可有效提升LOS/NLOS检测能力，并适合资源受限的实时室内定位系统部署。

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) direction-finding is promising for indoor industrial localization, but its accuracy degrades in multipath environments where reflections and scattering bias angle estimates. Although line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) detection is well studied for wide-band radios, BLE direction-finding still lacks narrow-band channel-feature representations, scalable kernel-based feature transformations, and dedicated datasets for data-driven, lightweight channel classification. To address this gap, the work introduces a controlled BLE measurement setup that generates labeled LOS/NLOS data in two distinct propagation environments. A quality-driven machine learning (ML)-based pipeline is then developed for BLE Constant Tone Extension (CTE) In-phase-Quadrature (IQ) features. First, robust quantile-based standardization is applied to reduce the influence of outliers and heavy-tailed effects. The standardized features are then analyzed using Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Adaptive Kernel Density Estimation (AKDE) to verify scenario-dependent statistics and reveal LOS/NLOS separability. Next, Nyström Kernel Approximation (NKA) constructs low-rank nonlinear feature maps followed by a lightweight Support Vector Classifier (SVC) head for LOS/NLOS detection. This classifier is compared with Random Forest (RF) and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) models. Results show that NKA improves accuracy by about 7-14% relative to the raw baseline. Although the MLP achieves higher absolute accuracy, the Nyström--SVC approach offers a more favorable trade-off between training complexity, inference cost, and memory footprint. Finally, several pipeline-calibrated posterior probabilities are utilized for cost-aware threshold selection and efficient real-time LOS/NLOS detection in resource-constrained localization systems.

</details>


### [233] [Parity Selection Rule for Information and Dissipation in Driven Steady States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19702)
*Mengqi Li,Lixin Li,Wensheng Lin,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转驱动线性非平衡稳态中对称信息量与熵产生之间难以建立紧等式的原因，提出一个宇称选择规则来解释二者的结构性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究希望在驱动稳态中建立快照互信息等对称信息指标与熵产生之间的紧密等式关系，但这类关系为何普遍缺失尚不清楚。作者关注旋转驱动线性非平衡稳态，试图揭示其背后的对称性约束。

Method: 作者分析弛豫矩阵与扩散矩阵在对易或非对易情形下的互信息、驱动反转宇称以及熵产生行为；利用旋转对称性推导互信息的偶性、各向同性下的驱动无关性、平面情形闭式值，以及熵产生关于驱动强度的二次形式；并讨论重尾各向同性稳定噪声下规则的鲁棒性和实验检验方案。

Result: 当弛豫矩阵与扩散矩阵对易时，两个时间切片之间的快照互信息在驱动反转下严格为偶函数；若二者不对齐，宇称破缺随对易子范数线性增长。完全各向同性时，互信息进一步与驱动无关，平面互信息约为0.145 nats。同一对齐条件下，熵产生严格随驱动二次增长，其系数可由矩阵迹与行列式闭式表示。该规则还适用于尾指数小于2的各向同性稳定噪声。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在旋转驱动线性非平衡稳态中，对称信息与熵产生分属偶、奇/耗散相关的不同结构扇区，因而不支持紧等式，只允许单侧热力学不确定性界。该宇称选择规则源于漂移项的旋转对称性，并具有实验可检验性。

Abstract: Tight equalities between symmetric information and entropy production in driven steady states remain elusive. We show that they are forbidden by a parity selection rule for rotation-driven linear nonequilibrium steady states. Whenever the relaxation and diffusion matrices commute, the snapshot mutual information between two time slices is exactly even under drive reversal, and parity violation rises linearly in the commutator norm when alignment is broken. Full isotropy strengthens this to drive-independence, and the planar mutual information takes the closed-form value of about 0.145 nats. Under the same alignment, the entropy production is exactly quadratic in the drive, and its prefactor admits an explicit closed form in the traces and determinant of the two matrices. The orthogonality of even and odd sectors leaves only one-sided thermodynamic-uncertainty bounds. The rule rests on the rotational symmetry of the drift alone and survives heavy-tailed isotropic stable noise with tail index below two, where variance-based bounds become vacuous. A falsifiable test is proposed on an electrical Brownian gyrator augmented for independent drive control with circuit-level stable-noise injection.

</details>


### [234] [Doeblin Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19859)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur,Japneet Singh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Doeblin 曲线，以更细粒度刻画马尔可夫核的多方信息收缩行为，即使传统 Doeblin 系数为 0 也能给出非空收缩保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Doeblin 系数虽可推广 Dobrushin 总变差收缩系数，但通常需要系数远离 0 才能证明信息收缩；对于 Doeblin 系数为 0 的信道，现有刻画往往失效。因此需要一种能描述不同散度水平和幂约束下收缩行为的非线性、更精细工具。

Method: 作者引入 Doeblin 曲线，作为量化马尔可夫核对一组输入分布在特定散度水平与功率条件下收缩行为的非线性函数；同时建立 Doeblin 系数的新变分表征，研究 Doeblin 曲线的性质，定义多种功率约束版本，并基于变分表征推导上下界。

Result: 该框架能够在传统 Doeblin 系数为 0 的情况下仍提供非平凡的信息收缩界；并被应用于带噪迭代优化的泛化界、带噪电路可靠计算的误差界，以及在线迭代算法的差分隐私保证，从而扩展了相关结果的适用范围或群组设置。

Conclusion: Doeblin 曲线为多方信息收缩提供了比 Doeblin 系数更细粒度的刻画工具，能够揭示传统系数无法捕捉的非线性收缩现象，并在优化、计算和隐私等问题中带来更强或更广泛的理论保证。

Abstract: Recent research on Doeblin coefficients has shed light on their usefulness as a multi-way generalization of the Dobrushin contraction coefficient for TV distance, in a separate vein from their classic role in the theory of Markov chain ergodicity. However, strong conditions, such as being bounded away from 0, are typically necessary for Doeblin coefficients to establish the existence of information contraction. Building on recently formulated concepts of nonlinear information contraction, we aim to propose a finer-grained Doeblin-based characterization of multi-way contraction behavior which yields non-vacuous contraction guarantees even for channels whose Doeblin coefficient is 0. To this end, we introduce the notion of a Doeblin curve -- a nonlinear function which quantifies the contraction behavior of a Markov kernel on collections of input distributions at specific levels of divergence and power. Through the course of our analysis, we develop a new variational characterization of Doeblin coefficients, present several properties of Doeblin curves, define several versions of power-constrained Doeblin curves, and derive upper and lower bounds using our aforementioned variational characterization. We then utilize these results in diverse areas, including generalization bounds for noisy iterative optimization, error bounds for reliable computation with noisy circuits, and differential privacy guarantees for online iterative algorithms. In particular, we extend results in these areas to broader domains or group settings, leveraging Doeblin curves to reveal finer-grained contraction phenomena than Doeblin coefficients.

</details>


### [235] [Site-Specific MIMO Channel Generation via Diffusion and Flow Matching: Fidelity, Efficiency, and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20098)
*Sina Beyraghi,Masoud Sadeghian,Firdous Bin Ismail,Angel Lozano,Paul Almasan,Giovanni Geraci*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文比较两类位置条件生成模型 cDDIM 与 cFMM，用于合成站点特定的高质量 MIMO 信道数据，以降低真实测量成本；结果表明二者均能有效保留空间与传播特性，其中 cFMM 在相近质量下推理速度约快一个数量级，并能显著提升下游物理层任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 原生无线网络需要大量真实、站点特定的 MIMO 信道数据，但大规模实地测量成本高、耗时长。论文希望通过生成模型在少量真实数据条件下合成可靠信道样本，缓解数据稀缺问题，并支持下游无线通信任务。

Method: 论文研究并比较两种以用户坐标为条件的生成范式：条件去噪扩散隐式模型 cDDIM 和条件流匹配模型 cFMM。二者均以用户位置作为条件输入，生成对应的 MIMO 信道矩阵，以保持部署场景的空间结构。评估维度包括统计保真度、生成效率，以及在 CSI 压缩和波束对齐等下游任务中的实用性。

Result: 在 28 GHz 与 3.5 GHz、视距与非视距等多种传播场景中，两种模型均能较好捕获站点特定信道特征，即使真实训练数据较少也表现稳定。cFMM 在生成质量上接近 cDDIM，但推理时间约减少一个数量级。使用合成信道扩充稀缺数据集后，下游任务性能明显优于仅使用少量真实数据或随机信道模型。

Conclusion: 位置条件生成模型可作为低成本构建站点特定 MIMO 信道数据集的有效工具。相比 cDDIM，cFMM 在质量与效率之间具有更优折中，尤其适合需要快速生成大量信道样本的 AI 原生无线网络场景。

Abstract: This paper explores the use of generative models to synthesize high-quality, site-specific multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel data, addressing the high cost of the extensive measurement campaigns required to acquire real-world data for AI-native wireless networks. Two location-conditioned generative paradigms are compared: a conditional denoising diffusion implicit model (cDDIM), and a conditional flow matching model (cFMM). Both these models generate MIMO channel matrices conditioned on user coordinates, to preserve the spatial structure of the deployment site. The approaches are evaluated across three dimensions: statistical fidelity (including beam consistency and effective rank), generation efficiency, and utility in downstream tasks such as channel-state information compression and beam alignment. Results across diverse propagation scenarios (28 GHz and 3.5 GHz, both line-of-sight and non-line-of-sight) demonstrate that both models accurately capture site-specific characteristics, even when trained on scarce ground-truth data. Notably, cFMM achieves a quality comparable to cDDIM with roughly an order of magnitude less inference time. Augmenting scarce site-specific datasets with these synthetic channels yields hefty performance gains in downstream physical layer tasks compared to using scarce data alone or stochastic channels.

</details>


### [236] [Spatially Robust Near-Field SWIPT Using Pinching Antennas: Rate-Energy Tradeoff Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20133)
*Zoran Hadzi-Velkov,Marija Poposka,Slavche Pejoski,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向服务区域覆盖的PWA-SWIPT鲁棒设计，通过离散天线选择提升近场信息与能量传输在用户位置偏移下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PWA近场优化多以单点最大增益为目标，对定位误差和用户移动高度敏感；近场信号在小尺度位移下波动明显，导致信息解码与能量采集性能不稳定。因此需要从点优化转向区域覆盖鲁棒优化。

Method: 论文建立面向ID与EH服务区域的鲁棒QoS模型，将问题表述为带二进制天线激活约束的非凸二次规划，在ID区域满足鲁棒速率约束并最大化EH区域采集能量；采用半定松弛给出R-E区域上界，并设计低复杂度基于交换的局部搜索算法获得满足二进制硬件约束的下界解。

Result: 数值结果表明，覆盖导向设计能够实现更鲁棒的速率-能量折中，并在服务区域内保持稳定性能，相比传统单点近场优化更能抵抗用户位移。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在PWA辅助SWIPT中，基于离散天线激活的服务区域覆盖设计比点目标优化更适合实际移动和定位不确定场景，并为其性能边界与可实现算法提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Pinching Waveguide Antennas (PWAs) offer significant potential for simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) by enabling precise near-field energy focusing. However, existing optimization frameworks are largely point-based (targeting a single coordinate for maximum gain), and thus highly sensitive to positioning errors and mobility, as near-field signals fluctuate significantly even over small spatial displacements. In this paper, we propose a spatially robust design framework based on discrete antenna selection optimized for service area (SA) coverage. Unlike point-based approaches, our model guarantees quality of service within predefined SAs for both information decoding (ID) and energy harvesting (EH) receivers, thereby improving robustness to user displacements. We formulate the problem as a non-convex binary quadratic program aimed at maximizing harvested energy within the EH SA subject to robust rate constraints in the ID SA. To characterize fundamental performance limits, we develop a semidefinite relaxation (SDR) framework that provides an upper bound on the achievable rate-energy (R-E) region. For the lower bound, we employ a low-complexity swap-based local search algorithm enforcing binary hardware constraints. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed coverage-oriented design yields a robust R-E tradeoff and maintains stable performance across service regions, highlighting the advantages of discrete antenna activation over point-based near-field optimization approaches.

</details>
